- `(pending)` P0P.G2: Automation layer — scheduled unattended jobs + reconcile alarm. Six commands registered in `routes/console.php`, all withoutOverlapping()+onOneServer(), all active-tenants-only: credentials:refresh-status (02:10), nursing:materialize-visits (02:20), clinical:evaluate-recalls (02:30, NEW command — none existed), billing:dunning-run (06:00), billing:reconcile (06:30 = launch-blocker monitor), appointments:dispatch-reminders (*/15, enqueue only). Hardening: credentials:refresh-status was iterating ALL tenants incl. suspended (fixed); billing:dunning-run/billing:reconcile took REQUIRED tenantId+actorId so could not run unattended — args now optional, absent = sweep active tenants via new `SystemActorResolver` (lowest-id tenant-wide permission holder; never super-admin, never branch-scoped, SKIP not escalate). Reconcile alarm (`ReconciliationAlarm`): failed run leaves the append-only reconciliation_runs row + an error-level log + the `billing.reconciliation.alarm` tenant setting (period/run id/failed invariants); clears only when the SAME period later passes; a failing tenant never aborts the sweep and the command exits non-zero. Added `SettingsService::forget()` (set(null) is not equivalent — 'array' coerces null back to []). Prod runner documented: cron `schedule:run` every minute + Horizon under supervisor; local Windows cannot keep Horizon alive (no pcntl) — use schedule:work + queue:work. No page props/routes/payloads touched. 431 tests / 2820 assertions.

Flow: ImportBatchService.upload (league/csv parse — BOM/quoting/delimiter-sniff/encoding-repair, NOT hand-rolled; private disk `tenants/{t}/imports/{ulid}.csv`, no public URL) → setMapping (column→field + explicit date format; required first_name/last_name/date_of_birth) → ImportValidator.validate (DRY-RUN: parse/validate per row, run existing DuplicateDetector, per-row status + batch summary, WRITES NOTHING) → ImportCommitter.commit (only on validated batch; creates via REAL PatientService incl contacts/identifiers/coverages — MRN/tenancy/validation/audit normal; duplicate policy default SKIP, else import_as_new / merge-via-audited-PatientMergeService; idempotent on batch+row status; one `patient.import.committed` audit event; chain verifies). - `(pending)` P0P.G9: Waitlist auto-fill workflow (offer, notify, accept-through-booking). Extends Modules\Scheduling. NEW `waitlist_offers` table (BelongsToTenant: waitlist_entry_id, source_appointment_id, patient_id, service_id, branch_id, slot_starts/ends_at, resource_ids JSON, status offered/accepted/declined/expired, offered_by, offered_at, expires_at, responded_at, booked_appointment_id) + `WaitlistOffer` model. `WaitlistOfferService`: candidates() reuses WaitlistService::matchingForSlot (service/branch/window, tenant-scoped); offer() creates a time-boxed hold (TTL = scheduling.waitlist.offer_ttl_minutes default 30; one open offer per entry) + fires WaitlistOfferLifecycleChanged; accept() books via EXISTING BookingService::book (no-double-book resource lock) then marks entry booked + offer accepted (expiry checked/recorded OUTSIDE the booking txn so the throw can't roll it back); decline()/expire()/expireDue() release the hold, entry stays waiting for the next candidate. Notify path composed in APP LAYER (listener on WaitlistOfferLifecycleChanged audits waitlist_offer.* and, on creation only, sends the new `waitlist.offer` built-in template — TRANSACTIONAL, consent-gated on comms.email, skips fail-closed without consent) via Comms NotificationService — Scheduling never imports Comms (arch test unchanged; D-073). `ExpireWaitlistOffersCommand` (scheduling:expire-waitlist-offers, every 5 min, withoutOverlapping+onOneServer, active tenants). Reception UI additive on the day-board (net-new): waitlistOffers prop + offer/candidates/accept/decline action URLs + a "Waitlist auto-fill" panel (find candidates → one-click offer → accept/decline; status visible); appointment summary gains service_id; no existing prop removed; i18n keys; RBAC appointment.manage on every endpoint. Tests: tests/Feature/Scheduling/WaitlistOfferTest.php (8) — cancel surfaces matching entries (service/branch/window, tenant-scoped); offer notifies consent-gated (skipped no_consent / sent); accept books via BookingService + entry booked + chain verifies; decline frees slot for next candidate; expired offer does not book; expire sweep; RBAC (billing role refused); tenant isolation + audited — plus WaitlistOfferHammerTest (1) AttemptOfferAcceptCommand × 6 processes on one freed slot → exactly one ACCEPTED, 5 CONFLICT, 1 appointment. composer check green; npm run build green.

- `(pending)` P0P.G15: MySQL 8 full-parity verification + portability sweep (D-081; verified top commit 364c4fa P0P.G14 before starting). CONFIRMATORY + hardening; found TWO real problems. (1) CI HAD BEEN RED SINCE P0P.G7 — 8 commits, unnoticed (gates ran local checks only). Triage via the GitHub API (job log of run 29591093078): 2 failures, both 429s in CheckInTest — NOT an engine bug. Root cause: CI exports CACHE_STORE=redis at the OS level and phpunit <env> cannot override OS env (same class as the P0G.G2 queue incident) → throttle counters persist in REAL Redis across tests → the kiosk rate-limit test exhausts the 10/min bucket for later kiosk tests. REPRODUCED LOCALLY on MariaDB with CACHE_STORE=redis (identical 2 failures → engine-independent). Fix: Cache::flush() per test in CheckInTest beforeEach (config-pinning the store is NOT enough — Fortify named limiters resolve the RateLimiter singleton at boot, freezing its store; documented in the file + DB-PARITY). Proven: green under CACHE_STORE=redis twice back-to-back + normal path. (2) REAL ENGINE DIVERGENCE found by the sweep: information_schema on MariaDB shows implicit ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP on 9 first-non-nullable-TIMESTAMP columns (MariaDB explicit_defaults_for_timestamp=OFF vs MySQL 8 ON). Six harmless (append-only, UPDATE trigger-blocked: ai_interactions/integrity_checks/messages/payment_allocations/reconciliation_runs/refunds). THREE reachable: patient_consents.granted_at (consent WITHDRAWAL rewrote the grant moment to now() on MariaDB, preserved on MySQL 8 — real data bug), portal_login_tokens.expires_at (rewritten on consumption), thread_reads.read_at (masked; every update sets it explicitly — a trap). Fix: 2 migrations (Patients 2026_07_17_000010: granted_at + expires_at → DATETIME; Comms 2026_07_17_300010: read_at → DATETIME) per the standing D-E4 rule. NEW tests/Feature/Platform/MutableMomentParityTest.php (3): withdrawal preserves granted_at + consumption preserves expires_at (both FAILED on MariaDB pre-fix, pass post-fix) + engine-independent information_schema guard (no non-append-only table may carry an on-update column — fails on whichever engine regresses). Sweep results (each verified): FULLTEXT ngram → try-WITH PARSER ngram/fallback in patients migration ✓; spatial → nullable location + NOT NULL location_index mirror for the SPATIAL index + ST_Distance_Sphere/ST_GeomFromText both engines (MariaDB ≥10.4.19) ✓; CHECK constraints (5) enforced identically both engines ✓; triggers all SIGNAL 45000, ImmutabilitySweepTest PASS on MySQL 8 in the CI log (all 15 protected tables + positive sides) ✓; audit_events RANGE partitioning builds in CI ✓; NO generated columns, NO enum columns ✓; charset/collation pinned utf8mb4/utf8mb4_unicode_ci in config ✓; sql_mode strict=true → identical explicit mode list both engines ✓. Fresh-MySQL8 migrate: CI "Run migrations" step SUCCESS on the clean mysql:8 service; NEW ci.yml step asserts ZERO PENDING via migrate:status (fails on "Pending"). NEW composer script test:mysql = migrate:fresh --force + migrate:status + full pest (manual re-verification against a THROWAWAY MySQL 8; documented). docs/DB-PARITY.md: engines, all 10 divergence classes + handling, one-step verification commands, CI-env-divergence playbook. MySQL 8 suite numbers: pre-fix CI run (P0P.G14 tree) = 2 failed, 575 passed (4090 assertions) — the two cache-env failures only, everything else incl. hammers/airplane/simulated-month/immutability green; post-fix G15 CI run checked after push (expected to match local). Local MariaDB composer check green (Pint, PHPStan L5 OK, Pest full suite incl. the 3 new parity tests).

PAYMENTS: record→PaymentService::record (methods bank_transfer/card/cash/other; NO PSP — records a receipt), allocate→::allocate, reverse→::reverseAllocation — every move APPEND-ONLY; the service's over-allocation / reversal guards are caught as InvalidArgumentException and surfaced as `withErrors`, NEVER reimplemented; remainder=::unallocated, open=::openBalance. HARD RULE held + adversarially verified by grep: NO financial math in any controller/view — every `_minor` is a service call or a model-attribute passthrough; the only view arithmetic is money-format `/100`, rate-format `*100` on a service-returned ratio, and major→minor INPUT normalisation. - `(pending)` FIX.1: resolve C-1 (QA-audit delivery-blocker) — billing + import DETAIL and WRITE routes 500'd in the real browser (`TenantContextMissingException` at route-model-binding time). ROOT: `IdentifyTenantFromUser` is `append`-ed to the web group in `bootstrap/app.php`, so it runs AFTER Laravel's `SubstituteBindings`; a controller that IMPLICITLY binds a `BelongsToTenant` model (`show(Invoice $invoice)`) resolves it during `SubstituteBindings` — before the tenant context exists — and the fail-closed global scope throws. FIX: converted 12 actions to `string $id` + in-controller `Model::query()->whereKey($id)->firstOrFail()` (the app-wide convention, cf. `PatientShowController`/`ClinicalChartController`): InvoiceController show/issue/creditNote/download, CreditNoteController show, PaymentController show/allocate/reverse, ImportBatchController show/mapping/validateBatch/commit (import was PRE-EXISTING). Missing/cross-tenant id → 404 (fail-closed preserved); routes/URLs + all downstream service calls byte-identical; NO billing/payment/dunning LOGIC changed. App-wide grep confirmed billing + import were the ONLY implicit-bound tenant models (every other detail controller already uses string ids). NEW `tests/Feature/RouteBindingTenantContextTest.php` (3 tests / 16 assertions) hits the routes with `TenantContext::forget()` after seeding (before the request) so `IdentifyTenantFromUser` establishes context the way a real request does — CONFIRMED it FAILS (500 at TenantScope.php:38) on the old code and PASSES on the fix, and asserts 404 for missing/cross-tenant. TEST-GAP LESSON: the W6/W7 feature tests pre-set the `TenantContext` singleton in fixtures, masking the request-time ordering (green CI, broken browser). Existing behavior tests UNCHANGED. VERIFIED: composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **601 passed / 4519 assertions**, 0 failed). (D-090)
- `(pending)` FIX.2: wire the staff landing (`/app`) to real MetricsService figures (M-1, from the QA audit) — the landing was an unwired placeholder ("—" / "Awaiting live data") despite real data, the first screen a customer sees. NEW `App\Http\Controllers\AppLandingController` (`GET /app`, replacing the prop-less closure) assembles a TODAY, tenant-wide view-model from the EXISTING `MetricsService` (P0P.G14): appointments (+ `by_status`), waiting (ARRIVED count), no-shows, active patients — all only with `reporting.view`; outstanding balance — only with `billing.view`. Each metric is called CONDITIONALLY on `Gate::allows` (MetricsService throws without the perm), so a role with neither (e.g. reception) gets the shell with both props `null` — NEVER a 500. NO new metric or query was invented — only existing service output, scoped to today + current tenant; money stays integer minor units. `App/Landing.vue` rebound: the glance tile + 4 KPI `StatCard`s (operational) + an Outstanding card (financial) + a today's-schedule strip (real count + status chips, or a genuine "Nothing scheduled today yet" at zero — distinct from the old "awaiting data" stub) + quick links. The landing date is parsed as LOCAL midnight (`T00:00:00`) so it never shifts a day. i18n keys only. NEW `tests/Feature/AppLandingTest.php` (3 tests / 71 assertions): real figures for a manager with data (assertInertia values), genuine zeros for an empty tenant (operational present, counts 0 — not the stub), and RBAC gating (coordinator → operational-only, billing role → financial-only, reception → both null, page still renders). Existing behavior tests UNCHANGED (AppShellTest's component-only + suspended-tenant-403 assertions still hold). VERIFIED: npm build green; composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **604 passed / 4590 assertions**, 0 failed). Wires the P.14 service; see [[Reporting]].

Findings: **no flow failed** — nothing fixed beyond M-2 scope. `docs/QA-AUDIT-REPORT.md` gains a "§7 FIX-verify" section (C-1 confirmed-in-browser table + M-2). `.playwright-mcp/` added to `.gitignore`. NEW scripts `composer test:smoke` + `npm run test:smoke` (local run); QA report gains a §8 documenting the guard. Maintainability: a single route list in the test — a new page is one line. Mapped first with 3 parallel Explore agents (booking/slot engine, branch references, tenant/audit/migration). **Profile:** NEW nullable `tenants` columns (contact_email/phone, address_line1/2, city, postal_code, country) via a Platform migration; editable through `SettingsController::updateProfile` (name + contact/address → tenant columns; locale + timezone → SettingsService). slug/region/status/plan stay READ-ONLY (slug = public `/book/{slug}` key, region immutable, status/plan platform/billing). **Timezone/locale applied:** NEW `App\Http\Middleware\ApplyTenantLocaleTimezone` (web group, after IdentifyTenantFromUser) runs `date_default_timezone_set()` + `app()->setLocale()` from the tenant settings — it NEVER touches `config('app.timezone')`, so Eloquent keeps serialising UTC (stored data unchanged); surfaced on Inertia's `locale`/`timezone` as LAZY closures (Inertia evaluates `share()` before the middleware, so eager values missed it). A tenant that set nothing keeps platform defaults → existing behaviour untouched. Full per-widget datetime→tz display flagged as a follow-up. **Branch CRUD:** NEW `branch_hours` table + `BranchHours` model (per-weekday, validated in booted like ResourceAvailability) + `BranchHoursService`; NEW **app-layer** `App\Http\Controllers\BranchController` + `App\Services\BranchService` — app layer because the deactivation guard spans Platform's Branch + Scheduling's appointments/resources and `arch('Platform does not depend on Scheduling')` forbids it inside Platform. **SCHEDULING SAFETY (two tested guards):** (1) deactivation is SOFT (`active=false`, never a hard delete — appointments/encounters/charges/visits `restrictOnDelete` a branch) and BLOCKED when the branch has future active appointments (blockingStatuses, starts_at ≥ now); the day-board + portal branch lists now filter `active=true` (public booking already did) so a deactivated branch vanishes from every booking surface. (2) opening hours feed the engine: `AvailableSlotFinder` bounds its scan to the branch's [open, close] for the weekday (closed day → no slots), and `BookingService::createBooking` (the authoritative funnel for book/bookOnline/series/waitlist) + `checkAvailability` reject a start outside hours (NEW `BookingUnavailableException::outsideBranchHours`). BACKWARD-COMPATIBLE: a branch with NO configured hours keeps the default 07:00–19:00 and imposes no booking constraint, so every existing scheduling test (none set hours) stays green. All writes admin.manage-gated, tenant-scoped (cross-tenant → 404), audited via app-layer model hooks (branch.created/updated/activated/deactivated, branch.hours_changed, tenant.profile_updated — Platform never imports Audit). NEW Vue: editable `Admin/Settings.vue` (profile + identity + billing + branch summary) + `Admin/Branches.vue` (create + per-branch details/hours-grid/deactivate); `branchesAdmin.*` i18n. GAP flagged: adding resources (rooms/chairs) to a branch has no backend, so a new branch isn't bookable until resources are seeded. NEW tests: `tests/Feature/Platform/BranchAndProfileTest.php` (10 — profile edit+audit+gate+validation, branch create/edit/code-unique/gate, **deactivation BLOCKED with future appts**, empty-branch deactivate removes from day-board, cross-tenant 404, hours saved+audited+invalid-window, timezone applied+surfaced) + `tests/Feature/Scheduling/BranchHoursSlotTest.php` (4 — unconfigured keeps default window, configured hours bound slots, closed day = no slots, booking rejected outside/accepted inside). DISCOVERY first (read the real code): AiCore `ApprovalQueue::approve/reject` (re-authorizes the reviewer against the TOOL's own permission + `assertPending`; reject executes nothing; approve runs only `tool->execute()`; both fire `AgentActionLifecycleChanged` → app-layer glue audits `agent_action.*`/`ai_interaction.*`), `AutonomyPolicy` (off/suggest/approve/auto, clinical/financial hard-capped at approve, applied at PROPOSE time), `AgentAction`; Audit `AuditService::verifyChain(?tenantId)` (pure replay, writes nothing) + `AuditEvent` (read-only, **NO `BelongsToTenant`** — Audit may not depend on Platform); Platform `IntegrityCheck` (D-069) + `Setting`/`KillSwitch`; Billing `ReconciliationRun` (D-068) + `ReconciliationEngine::check` (pure read); permission catalog (`audit.view`/`ai.manage` exist, org_admin holds both); + the `Governance Dashboard`/`Admin Approval Queue` prototypes (extracted labels, not markup). Both controllers live in the APP LAYER (`App\Http\Controllers\GovernanceDashboardController` + `AiApprovalQueueController`) because they compose Audit+Platform+Billing+AiCore. **PART A — Governance (`/governance`, `audit.view`, STRICTLY READ-ONLY):** displays posture from EXISTING data — live `verifyChain()` + latest scheduled `IntegrityCheck`; latest `ReconciliationRun` + persisted `billing.reconciliation.alarm`; AI-usage outcome counts + integer-minor cost over the append-only `ai_interactions` ledger (30-day window); pending-`AgentAction` depth; kill-switch state (via `KillSwitch::enabled()`); recent + security-relevant audit events (DISPLAYED, never editable). NO mutation path (every source append-only at model+trigger; controller only reads); the one POST ("verify now") re-runs the existing verification and appends NOTHING (tested: audit-event count unchanged). **`AuditEvent` filtered by `tenant_id` EXPLICITLY** (no auto-scope) — the isolation guarantee (tested cross-tenant). **PART B — AI approval-queue (`/governance/approvals`, `ai.manage`, READ + ACT-THROUGH-EXISTING-PATH):** lists PENDING agent actions (agent/tool/category/autonomy/why/source-grounded proposed output, AI-draft badged); approve/reject go ONLY through `ApprovalQueue` — NO new execute path, NO create/propose route (a human can't inject an un-fenced action; the fence refuses clinical asks at propose time), NEVER sets autonomy (the request body can't raise it — tested). THE CAP BINDS: `ApprovalQueue` re-authorizes the reviewer against the tool's own permission, so a reviewer with `ai.manage` but lacking a tool's permission (e.g. `appointment.manage`) is DENIED (403 — the controller lets `AuthorizationException` propagate and catches only `AiCoreException`); reject executes nothing; every decision is audited by the EXISTING glue (controller adds no audit). Actions resolve by STRING id (FIX.1/D-090) → cross-tenant/missing = 404. NEW `Governance/Dashboard.vue` + `Governance/ApprovalQueue.vue` (Eucalyptus Glow — StatCards, glass cards, status badges, per-card approve/reject-with-reason), two nav entries (`audit.view`/`ai.manage`, added to `HandleInertiaRequests::NAV_PERMISSIONS`), `governance.*`+`aiQueue.*` i18n. NEW tests: `tests/Feature/Governance/GovernanceDashboardTest.php` (4 — read-only+real-status+gated, tenant-scoped activity isolation, verify-now writes-nothing + no mutation route + model immutable, non-audit-viewer denied) + `AiApprovalQueueTest.php` (4 — lists pending+gated+tenant-scoped+no-create-route, approve-through-existing-path+audited+autonomy-not-raisable, reject-does-nothing+reason-required+audited, cap-binds-via-UI + cross-tenant 404). Route smoke gains both GET routes (org_admin 200 / reception 403). Completeness fix in passing: `AgentAction` `@property` block gained the real review-timestamp/reason columns (approved_at/rejected_at/executed_at/rejection_reason — in fillable+casts but undocumented, so L5 flagged them) — same as W8's Branch fix, docblock only, no behavior. ONE existing test updated (tracking, not weakening — same category as W8's admin.manage add): `NavAndErrorPageTest`'s exact nav-permissions map gained `audit.view`+`ai.manage` (the two new nav links are gated on them). The P.4 eval harness + audit/integrity/immutability suites are UNCHANGED and green. VERIFIED: npm build green; `composer test:smoke` green; composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **649 passed / 4902 assertions**, 0 failed). Closes two founder-scope admin gaps (governance + AI approval-queue); remaining unwired: KB admin, staff-telehealth join. See [[D-097]], [[AiCore]], [[Platform]].

`DentalServiceProvider` boot=loadMigrationsFrom; composer psr-4 + bootstrap/providers; arch: `Dental may use care modules + Audit services but not Audit models, AiCore, Nursing, or Comms`). **Tooth notation = FDI/ISO 3950** two-digit (`Support\ToothNotation`, pure) — permanent 11–18/21–28/31–38/41–48 (32) + primary 51–55/61–65/71–75/81–85 (20, family dentist charts children); dentition DERIVED from the id (never stored); a patient's tooth set = whatever teeth have records (missing = a charted state, mixed dentition = both — no hardcoded 32-tooth assumption). **`tooth_records`** (BelongsToTenant, **APPEND-ONLY** at model `updating`/`deleting`-throw + DB triggers `tooth_records_no_update`/`_no_delete` SIGNAL-45000 portable): one immutable charting row per tooth-or-surface moment — `tooth` (FDI), `surface` (nullable, null=whole-tooth), `charted_condition` (a fact the dentist SELECTED from an allowed vocabulary per scope: WHOLE_TOOTH present/missing/unerupted/implant/pontic/crown/root_canal/bridge_retainer vs SURFACE sound/caries/restoration/fracture/sealant/veneer/erosion/abrasion), `note`, `reason` (a correction is a NEW row + reason, prior states never destroyed), `charted_by`, `charted_at`. **current odontogram = latest per (tooth, surface); history = every row.** **ELECTRIC FENCE (record-not-judge, same as vitals D-D3 / order results D-076):** DELIBERATELY no severity/score/risk/grade/abnormal/flag/priority/recommendation column — the system records what the dentist charts, never detects caries / grades decay / assesses risk / diagnoses (asserted by a schema + recursive-output fence test). `Services\ToothChartService` pure record+retrieve: `chart()` (Gate `dental.chart`, actor+patient same-tenant fail-closed, deterministic FDI/surface/condition validation, audited `dental.tooth_charted`), `currentChart()`/`history()` (Gate `patient.view`, patient-scoped `read` audit). Append-only: a correction is a NEW performed record; the prior is preserved + the model is immutable (tested at model + raw-DB). **UI:** the G2 odontogram extended ADDITIVELY — a "Perform a procedure" side-panel form (procedure + branch + optional resulting tooth-state + note, shown only when `can_perform` = dental.chart && billing.manage) + a per-tooth performed-procedure history; `OdontogramController::perform` (POST `/dental/chart/{patient}/perform`, string-id FIX.1). NO new billing/charting logic — adversarial grep still finds zero pricing/charge/VAT math in `Modules\Dental`; G1 append-only preserved. The dentist builds a phased plan whose estimate REUSES the G3 fee schedule (snapshotted at proposal), estimates WITHOUT billing (G4 performing charges), and links performed procedures back to plan items to track completion. Ties G3 (pricing) + G4 (perform). Per `docs/DENTAL-DELIVERY-MAP.md`; critical path G1→G5. DISCOVERY first (confirmed the G3 `DentalProcedure->tariffItem->unit_price_minor` fee path, the `ServiceAgreementService` legal-transition state-machine pattern, and G4's `PerformProcedureService::perform` signature). **Built (domain + service + UI):** NEW `treatment_plans` (BelongsToTenant, LogsReads, NOT append-only — a lifecycle) → `treatment_plan_phases` → `treatment_plan_items` (a planned procedure = a G3 `dental_procedure` + tooth/surface + `estimated_fee_minor`); + additive nullable `performed_procedures.treatment_plan_item_id` (ALTER is DDL — the G4 append-only UPDATE trigger doesn't fire). `Services\TreatmentPlanService`: `create/addPhase/addItem` (draft-only, gate `dental.chart`, sequence=max+1); **`propose` SNAPSHOTS each item's `estimated_fee_minor` from the live tariff fee (READ through the existing store — no recompute) then transitions draft→proposed — so a later fee-schedule edit NEVER changes an accepted plan's agreed estimate (tested UP-edit → estimate unchanged)**; `accept`(stamps accepted_at)/`decline`/`start`/`complete` via a legal-only `transition`/`canTransition` match machine (illegal throws `DentalException`; completed/declined terminal — tested draft→accept + completed→start + declined→accept all throw); `itemEstimate` = snapshot ?? live fee; phase/plan totals = `->sum(itemEstimate)` (the ONLY arithmetic — adversarial grep finds NO pricing/charge/VAT/discount math in `Modules\Dental`, only pass-throughs + this sum). **NO DOUBLE-CHARGE (tested): the plan ESTIMATES; accept/start post ZERO charges** — a charge is created ONLY when the procedure is PERFORMED (G4). **Link to G4:** `PerformProcedureService::perform` gained an optional `?TreatmentPlanItem $planItem = null` (G4's atomic 3-write workflow otherwise UNCHANGED — all existing G4 calls/tests unaffected) that validates same-tenant + `planItem->treatmentPlan->patient_id === patient->id` and stamps the link; a plan item is "done" when a performed procedure references it (DERIVED, no stored flag). **ELECTRIC FENCE: dentist-authored** — no auto-suggested procedures, no severity prioritisation, no AI; the service records what the dentist adds + sums fees; the rendered payload carries no severity/score/grade/risk/suggested/ai field (recursive `tpAssertNoJudgment`). **RBAC:** manage = `dental.chart`; read = `patient.view`; perform a planned item = dental.chart + billing.manage (via G4). **UI:** `Http\Controllers\TreatmentPlanController` + `Dental/TreatmentPlans.vue` (`/dental/plans/{patient}`, string-id FIX.1) staff editor (build phases/items draft-only, lifecycle buttons mirroring the server machine, per-item perform-a-planned-item form) + `PortalTreatmentPlanController` + `Portal/TreatmentPlan.vue` (`/portal/treatment-plan`) read-only patient view (proposed-onward only, per-plan `auditRead`, no actions/PSP). PRESENTATIONAL (P0D.GU) — the service owns all logic. NEW `treatmentPlan.*`+`portalTreatmentPlan.*` i18n. **PHPStan L5 forced:** a `default => throw` arm on the validated-action `match` (mixed subject); `tariffItem->` not `tariffItem?->` (Larastan types the belongsTo non-null — `nullsafe.neverNull`). NEW `tests/Feature/Dental/TreatmentPlanTest.php` (5 — estimate snapshot-at-propose + fee-edit-doesn't-change-accepted; no-double-charge [accept/start = 0 charges] + perform links item + marks done; legal-only lifecycle; RBAC [reception can't author / billing can't read] + no-judgment fence; tenant-scoped cross-tenant 404). Route smoke gains `/dental/plans/{patient}` (doctor 200 / billing 403) + `/portal/treatment-plan`. NO existing behavior changed; the reconciliation/immutability/fence/eval + G1–G4 suites stay green UNCHANGED. VERIFIED: npm build green (new TreatmentPlans + Portal/TreatmentPlan chunks); composer test:smoke green; composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **681 passed / 5371 assertions**, 0 failed). **With G5 the CORE DENTAL SPINE (G1→G5) is COMPLETE — a general dentist can chart the mouth (G1/G2) → record + bill procedures (G3/G4) → build/present/track a phased fee-scheduled plan (G5). Remaining: G6 perio · G7 diagnosis record · G8 imaging (+ later sterilization/inventory, ortho/scan-compare, live imaging, licensed code sets).** See [[D-103]], [[Dental]], [[Billing]].
- `(pending)` DENTAL.G6: perio charting (per-site raw measurements, record-not-judge). Per-tooth, per-SITE periodontal measurements as RAW recorded facts — the vitals discipline applied to perio. Per `docs/DENTAL-DELIVERY-MAP.md`. DISCOVERY first (mirrored the exact G1 append-only clinical-model pattern: `tooth_records` migration [ULID pk, char tenant_id, denormalized patient_id, DB SIGNAL-45000 no-update/no-delete triggers] + `ToothRecord` [BelongsToTenant, HasUlids, LogsReads, `creating` validation, `updating`/`deleting`-throw] + `ToothChartService` [gate + assertActorTenant/assertPatientTenant fail-closed + audit + patient-scoped read-log] + `OdontogramController` [string-id `whereKey->firstOrFail`, Inertia render] + the recursive no-judgment fence test). **Built:** NEW `perio_exams` (BelongsToTenant, LogsReads, **APPEND-ONLY** at model + DB triggers `perio_exams_no_update`/`_no_delete`) — a point-in-time 6-point probing session (patient, examined_by, exam_date, note); groups NEW `perio_measurements` (BelongsToTenant, **APPEND-ONLY** model + triggers) — one row per tooth × SITE. **Six sites/tooth** (`PerioMeasurement::SITES` = mesio_buccal/buccal/disto_buccal/mesio_lingual/lingual/disto_lingual — the standard 6-point probing, DISTINCT from the odontogram's 5 anatomical `ToothNotation::SURFACES`). Per site the RAW probed values: `pocket_depth_mm` (unsignedTinyInt), `recession_mm` (smallInt — signed, negative = gingival overgrowth), `bleeding_on_probing` (bool), + optional per-tooth `mobility` (0–3) / `furcation` (0–4). Tooth = FDI (reuses G1 `ToothNotation::isValid`). **CRITICAL ELECTRIC FENCE (perio's core risk): RAW numbers ONLY — DELIBERATELY NO periodontal stage (I–IV), NO grade (A–C), NO severity, NO risk score, NO "disease detected", NO auto-flag of a deepening site, NO computed attachment-loss finding — in schema, service, OR UI.** `PerioMeasurement::assertValid` is PURE data-entry validation (valid FDI id, valid site, physically-plausible bounds: depth 0–15, recession -15–30, mobility 0–3, furcation 0–4) exactly like the odontogram rejecting an unknown surface — bounds reject impossible input, they NEVER grade. A per-site trend over time (`siteHistory`, oldest first) is RAW context — raw numbers in sequence, NO band/arrow/"worsening" label (same rule as unified vitals trends P.13). Proven by a recursive `pcAssertNoJudgment` (forbids stage/staging/grade/severity/risk/flag/classification/worsening/… keys) over BOTH the page props AND the siteHistory output. `Services\PerioChartService`: `recordExam` (gate `dental.chart`, actor+patient same-tenant fail-closed, `DB::transaction` of exam + its site rows — an invalid value throws and the WHOLE exam rolls back, audited `dental.perio_charted`); `examsFor`/`siteHistory` (gate `patient.view`, patient-scoped `read` audit). **UI:** `Http\Controllers\PerioChartController` (GET `/dental/perio/{patient}` = show, POST = store, string-id FIX.1) + `Dental/PerioChart.vue` — the classic perio grid (teeth × 6 sites entry with depth/recession/BOP + per-tooth mobility/furcation; prior exams as raw grids), PRESENTATIONAL (P0D.GU): NO severity colouring, NO flagged sites, NO stage/grade badge, NO auto-watch — a dot marks BOP (data entry), not severity. NEW `perio.*` i18n. Route smoke gains `/dental/perio/{patient}` (doctor 200 / billing 403). NEW `tests/Feature/Dental/PerioChartTest.php` (7 — records raw per-site measurements + tenant/patient scoped + audited + read-logged; append-only re-exam preserves prior [model AND raw-DB triggers on BOTH tables]; page payload carries NO stage/grade/severity/flag [the fence]; per-site history is raw over time + no judgment; rejects invalid FDI/site/out-of-range [deterministic, tx rolls back]; RBAC dental.chart-records/patient.view-reads; tenant-scoped cross-tenant fail-closed). NO existing behavior changed (no existing test modified; the helper prefix is `pc` to avoid a global collision with PerformProcedureTest's `pp` helpers); the reconciliation/immutability/fence/eval + G1–G5 suites stay green UNCHANGED. VERIFIED: npm build green (new PerioChart chunk); composer test:smoke green (3 passed); composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **688 passed / 5498 assertions**, 0 failed). Next: G7 diagnosis record · G8 imaging. See [[D-104]], [[Dental]], [[Clinical]].

Per `docs/DENTAL-DELIVERY-MAP.md`. DISCOVERY first (reused the G6 append-only clinical-model pattern: `tooth_records`/`perio_*` migration + model [BelongsToTenant, HasUlids, LogsReads, `creating` validation, `updating`/`deleting`-throw, DB SIGNAL-45000 triggers] + service [gate + fail-closed tenant asserts + audit + patient-scoped read-log] + controller [string-id `whereKey->firstOrFail`, Inertia] + the recursive no-judgment fence test; confirmed the delivery-map says build the prototype's AI-diagnosis WITHOUT the AI). **Built:** NEW `diagnoses` (BelongsToTenant, LogsReads, **APPEND-ONLY** model + DB triggers `diagnoses_no_update`/`_no_delete`) — stores what the DENTIST decided: `label` (text they wrote OR picked), optional `tooth`/`surface` (FDI, reuses `ToothNotation`), `findings`, `reason` (a change), and `status` ∈ `Diagnosis::STATUSES` {provisional, confirmed, ruled_out} the DENTIST sets; `diagnosis_term_id` = PROVENANCE only (null = free text). NEW `diagnosis_terms` (BelongsToTenant, plain catalog, NOT append-only) = the tenant's OWN pick-list `{label, is_active}` — TENANT-AUTHORED like the procedure catalog, **NO licensed diagnostic code set (ICD/SNODENT) bundled**. **CRITICAL ELECTRIC FENCE — THE SHARPEST (do not compromise): NO AI in the diagnosis path AT ALL this gate — NO suggested/proposed diagnosis, NO auto-ranked differential, NO computed likelihood/confidence, NOTHING auto-populates a diagnosis from the charting/perio/imaging.** The system ONLY records what the dentist entered; `status` is the DENTIST'S determination — recorded, never decided/suggested. The prototype's "agent's proposed diagnosis / auto-ranked differential" is built WITHOUT it (purely dentist-authored; a governed-AI diagnosis draft was DELIBERATELY not added — a diagnosis is the one place we want no AI in the loop for now). `Diagnosis::assertValid` is PURE data-entry validation (non-empty label, valid FDI/surface, known status) — never infers/ranks. The pick-list is a plain ALPHABETICAL list (`orderBy('label')`), NEVER sorted/filtered by a computed judgment. `Services\DiagnosisService`: `record` (gate `dental.chart`, actor+patient same-tenant fail-closed, term-id must be THIS tenant's [else DentalException], audited `dental.diagnosis_recorded`); `diagnosesFor` (gate `patient.view`, patient-scoped `read` audit, history = every row newest-first); `terms`/`addTerm` (the tenant's pick-list; addTerm audited `dental.diagnosis_term.created`). NO suggestion/ranking/inference method exists — adversarial grep finds zero suggest/propose/differential/likelihood/rank/AI logic in `Modules\Dental` diagnosis code. **UI:** `Http\Controllers\DiagnosisController` (GET `/dental/diagnoses/{patient}` = show, POST = store, POST `/dental/diagnosis-terms` = storeTerm, string-id FIX.1) + `Dental/Diagnoses.vue` — the dentist writes/picks a diagnosis, sets the status THEY determine, ties an optional tooth, references findings, manages their own term list; diagnosis history newest-first. PRESENTATIONAL (P0D.GU): NO "suggested diagnosis" UI, NO differential ranking, NO AI panel, NO auto-fill from charting. NEW `diagnosis.*` i18n. Route smoke gains `/dental/diagnoses/{patient}` (doctor 200 / billing 403). NEW `tests/Feature/Dental/DiagnosisTest.php` (6 — dentist records a diagnosis + tenant/patient scoped + audited + read-logged + dentist sets the status; **NOTHING auto-suggested/proposed/ranked/auto-populated** [the strictest fence: recursive `dxAssertNoSuggestion` over the payload+terms PLUS charting caries (G2) + probing 9mm perio pockets (G6) yields ZERO diagnoses]; append-only change preserves history [model AND raw-DB]; the tenant list is a plain pick-list [alphabetical, no rank/likelihood/score attr] + free text works + cross-tenant term id refused; RBAC dental.chart-records/patient.view-reads; tenant-scoped cross-tenant fail-closed). Helper prefix `dx` (no global collision). NO existing behavior changed (no existing test modified); the reconciliation/immutability/fence/eval + G1–G6 suites stay green UNCHANGED. - `(pending)` UI.F1: visual-fidelity — fix root token/shared-component drift to match Eucalyptus Glow prototype. PURELY VISUAL (P0D.GU) — no data/props/logic/route/fence/RBAC/billing change; NO omitted behaviour reintroduced (no graded vitals, AI panels, pay buttons, editable signed notes); NO .vue template/prop change, so every assertInertia/behaviour test passes UNCHANGED. **Opening:** top commit `dbbb6a3` DENTAL.G9 (the brief said `8e58acf` — flagged the hash drift per AGENTS.md; content is correct, DENTAL.G9 is on top); CI green. **DIAGNOSIS (rendered prototype `resources/prototype/*.html` [gitignored 52 MB bundles, served locally on :8001] vs the live app, comparing computed styles):** the drift is ROOT/shared, not per-page. **(1) THE root cause — no webfont delivered.** `resources/css/app.css` names `--font-sans: 'Inter'` and `<body class="font-sans">`, but nothing loaded Inter — no `@font-face`, no `<link>`, no font package (`grep` clean; zero woff2 in the repo). So the app rendered Inter ONLY on machines where Inter is system-installed and fell back to `ui-sans-serif, system-ui` (Segoe UI / SF / Roboto) everywhere else — shifting type metrics + vertical rhythm (hence "spacing/fonts feel off") on EVERY page, while the prototype always loads Inter via Google Fonts. (On the dev machine Inter happened to be system-installed, masking it — confirmed the app fetched zero font files.) **(2) `.glass-card` token drift:** shadow `0 14px 40px rgba(53,70,47,0.11)` vs the prototype's dominant `0 16px 44px …0.11` (18 uses); white hairline border opacity `0.6` vs the prototype's brighter `0.8`. **(3) `.euca-wash` glow:** top-right radial `rgba(198,218,191,0.5)` vs the prototype `0.55`. **Confirmed NON-drift (the colours are NOT wrong):** the euca ramp (`#f7faf5`→`#35462f`), ink (`#2a332a`), surfaces, radii (2xl = 20px), card blur (24px), and the shell/nav/pill structure all MATCH the prototype exactly — the "colours feel off" perception was downstream of the wrong font + the subtle card/wash drift. Root shared surfaces = the typography base (JS entry) + the shared `.glass-card` and `.euca-wash` classes. **FIX (root, shared — corrects every page + all 3 shells [app/auth/portal] at once, since all use `euca-wash`/`glass-card` and one JS entry):** (a) self-host Inter via `@fontsource/inter@^5` (weights 400/500/600/700 = every `font-*` utility the app uses), imported in `resources/js/app.ts` → Vite bundles 28 subset woff2 (`font-display: swap`) into the build — CSP-safe, NO external CDN, guaranteed Inter on every machine (verified: the live app now fetches its own `inter-latin-400-normal…woff2`); (b) `.glass-card` shadow → `0 16px 44px`, border opacity → `0.8` (verified computed 16px/44px + `0.8`, matching the prototype); (c) `.euca-wash` glow → `0.55`. **Re-comparison:** the shared fixes propagate to every page; on the dev machine (Inter pre-installed) the before/after is subtle, but the cross-machine win is decisive (Inter now always renders) and the glass cards now match the prototype's lift + hairline. **Remaining → F2 (page-specific):** per-page heading sizes (page h1 24px vs the prototype's 22px), the native `<input type=date>` styling, and the prototype's nav tenant-chip (that is CONTENT, not styling; the nav DENSITY difference is RBAC content — org_admin sees 13 items vs the prototype's curated 6 — not a visual-token fix). New dep `@fontsource/inter` in package.json + package-lock. NO test file changed. VERIFIED: npm run build green (28 woff2 emitted); composer check FULLY green (Pest 707/5741 unchanged); composer test:smoke green. See [[D-108]].

NO test file changed. VERIFIED: npm run build green; composer check FULLY green (Pest 707/5741 unchanged); composer test:smoke green. With F2 the UI matches the Eucalyptus Glow prototype at both the root (F1) and per-page (F2) level; what still differs is correct behavioural content, not visual drift. See [[D-109]], [[D-108]].

- `(pending)` HOSPITAL.G1: bed/ward/unit model + concurrency-safe bed-claim + inpatient RBAC (Phase 1 foundation). The FIRST hospital-vertical gate (inpatient/ADT), built per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE1-ADT-MAP.md` — the domain foundation everything else (ADT stay, ward board, charting, bed-billing, discharge) depends on. **Opening:** top commit `59ca4ae` (the ADT map), CI green. **NEW module `Modules\Hospital`** (PSR-4 + provider registered; arch rule mirrors Dental — may use care modules + Audit **services**, never Audit models/AiCore/Nursing/Comms; in G1 it depends only on the Platform foundation). **Bed is NET-NEW, NOT a Scheduling Resource** (map §2.1): a bed is occupied CONTINUOUSLY for a multi-day stay, so there is deliberately NO `starts_at`/`ends_at` — it reuses Resource's *pattern* (tenant + one branch + typed enum + `active`) and the `BookingService` lock idiom, not its table. **Ward is a Hospital-owned model** (documented choice — not Platform's unwired `Department` stub): clean bidirectional `Ward hasMany Bed`, inpatient-specific growth, the `Nursing\Visit → Branch` pattern; `Department` left for its generic purpose. **Housekeeping status** {free, occupied, cleaning, blocked} is a legal-only state machine (`Bed::TRANSITIONS`); **free→occupied is reached ONLY via the concurrency-safe `BedService::claim()`** (`DB::transaction` + `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` + assert-still-free), proven by `BedClaimParallelHammerTest` (8 OS processes race one free bed → exactly 1 CLAIMED / 7 CONFLICT — the `BookingParallelHammer` sibling). Each transition fires `BedStatusChanged` → an **app-layer listener** → one append-only `bed.status_changed` audit row; ward/bed CRUD audited via app-layer model hooks (Hospital stays free of Audit — the Branch/Resource + Appointment pattern). **Inpatient RBAC additive** (the `dental.chart` precedent; `Gate::before` unchanged): +4 permissions (`ward.manage`, `bed.manage`, `admission.manage`, `document.view`) + 6 role templates (ward_nurse, charge_nurse, hospitalist, bed_manager, admissions_clerk, him_records); `org_admin` gains all four; management gates on `bed`/`ward.manage`, the claim on `admission.manage`; ward-level scope is branch-level for Phase 1. **ELECTRIC FENCE (operational, not clinical):** a bed/ward has NO patient/acuity/severity/score/flag column — status is housekeeping, never a judgment (schema fence test); a NEWS2-style deterioration score is out (certified-partner/non-goal per the map §3). NO ADT workflow (G2), NO UI (G3). No existing behavior test modified; the relative permission-count assertion + the hardcoded RBAC negative-sweep stay green. Added tests: `WardBedManagementTest` (7 — CRUD+tenant/branch scoping+audit, legal status transitions, occupied→cleaning→free turnover, claim+conflict, RBAC gating, cross-tenant isolation, fence-no-judgment) + `BedClaimParallelHammerTest` (1, concurrency) + the Hospital arch rule (1). VERIFIED: `composer check` FULLY green (Pint `passed` · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · **Pest 717 passed / 2 skipped / 5841 assertions**, 0 failed — +9 tests/+70 assertions vs POLISH.3's 708/5771); no frontend this gate. See [[D-113]].
- `(pending)` HOSPITAL.G2: ADT stay + admit/transfer/discharge state machine (atomic, bed-safe). The CORE of the inpatient vertical — a patient is admitted to a bed, transferred between beds/wards, and discharged, over a multi-day `Stay`. Built per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE1-ADT-MAP.md`. **Opening:** top commit `99bc7f7` (HOSPITAL.G1), CI green. **`Stay` is a NET-NEW entity ABOVE an UNMODIFIED `Encounter`** (map §2.2 — the `VisitPlan→Visit` analogue; G2 does NOT touch Clinical, so Encounter's one-open-per-practitioner invariant holds for every vertical — bedside charting reuses Encounter per ward-round in G4). `stays` (BelongsToTenant, **LogsReads** patient-scoped read-log) is the MUTABLE current state (patient, admitting clinician [StaffProfile], current bed/ward, admitted_at/discharged_at, status, admission_type/reason, discharge_disposition); the immutable admit/transfer/discharge history is **append-only `stay_events`** (model + DB triggers `stay_events_no_update`/`_no_delete`, the tooth_records posture — the full bed journey is preserved). **State machine (legal-only):** {admitted → discharged}; a **transfer is a bed-move WITHIN admitted** (not a status change). `admission_type` ∈ {elective, emergency, transfer} = an operational ROUTE (validated in the model `creating` hook). **`AdmissionService` (gated `admission.manage`, tenant+branch fail-closed, rejects a cross-tenant bed UP FRONT):** admit / transfer / discharge are each **ATOMIC** (the dental-perform discipline) — the stay change + the bed claim/release (**via G1's proven concurrency-safe `BedService::claim`/`release` — NOT reimplemented**) + the append-only `StayEvent` all in ONE `DB::transaction`; a forced failure rolls back EVERYTHING (no orphan stay, no stuck bed — even the bed's audit row rolls back, proven by a test). admit `claim`s the bed (free→occupied); transfer `claim`s the new + `release`s the old (occupied→cleaning); discharge `release`s the bed + sets disposition/discharged_at. **One-active-stay guard** (patient row-lock + `lockForUpdate()->exists()` — the one-open-encounter analogue). Concurrency reuses G1's hammer-proven claim (admit-to-occupied-bed → conflict, tested). Every transition fires `StayTransitioned` → an **app-layer listener** → one append-only `admission.<eventType>` audit row (keyed by event type since a transfer keeps status=admitted; the AppointmentTransitioned pattern — Hospital stays free of Audit). `BedService` gained `release()` (occupied→cleaning, `admission.manage`, same lock idiom). **NO charge posted — bed-to-billing is G6.** Minimal action surface: `AdmissionController` (string-id FIX.1) — show (GET, `patient.view`, read-logged, renders a minimal `Hospital/Admission.vue`) + store/transfer/discharge (POST, `admission.manage`); FIX.5 route smoke extended (org_admin GET 200, reception POST admit 403). **ELECTRIC FENCE (operational, not clinical):** no acuity/severity/score/triage/deterioration column on stays/stay_events (schema fence test); the bed/ward/route/disposition are facts a human sets. NO ward-board/charting UI (G3+). No existing behavior test modified (the smoke was EXTENDED with the ADT fixture+routes, the ImportBatch precedent); Encounter untouched (asserted: an admit creates no Encounter). Added tests: `HospitalAdmissionTest` (12 — admit/transfer/discharge + audit, legal-only state machine, one-active-stay, three atomicity proofs incl. post-claim rollback, RBAC gating, cross-tenant isolation, append-only history, fence). VERIFIED: npm run build green; composer check FULLY green (Pint `passed` · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · **Pest 729 passed / 2 skipped / 5921 assertions**, 0 failed — +12 tests/+80 assertions vs G1's 717/5841); composer test:smoke green (3). See [[D-114]].

**the one-open-per-practitioner invariant is enforced UNCHANGED — a second round for the same stay is refused, tested**) + a `WardRound` link + a sign-and-lock note DRAFT (`ClinicalNoteService::saveDraft`), atomically, then the controller redirects into the **EXISTING note editor** (`clinical.notes.edit` — the write→sign→lock→amend→version flow is REUSED, not rebuilt). `recordVital` reuses `ClinicalListService::recordVital` (note.write) tied to the stay's latest round Encounter (raw `Vital`). **The ONLY new affordance is `vitalsForStay`** — a stay-scoped READ filtering the existing `Vital` store to the stay's round Encounters + building the RAW per-metric series via the existing `VitalsSeries::build` (NO schema change, no bands/flags/scores). `placeOrder` reuses `OrderService::place` (order.manage) tied to the round; `startRound`/`recordVital`/`placeOrder` (POST, the existing clinical gates). **RBAC = the EXISTING clinical permissions the inpatient roles already hold** (ward_nurse + hospitalist have encounter.manage/note.write/note.sign/order.manage; read = patient.view) — no new permission. **ELECTRIC FENCE carries through:** raw vitals (VitalsSeries), sign-and-lock notes unchanged, append-only order results — NO computed acuity/deterioration/early-warning score (NEWS2 = certified-partner/non-goal, NOT built); the stay-chart payload carries no judgment field (recursive scan). **No charge posted (billing is G6).** No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED with the stay-chart route — doctor 200 / billing 403); **Clinical's suite + Encounter's invariant tests stay green** (reuse, not modify). The write is audited via an app-layer `Handover::created` hook (`handover.recorded`) — Hospital stays free of Audit. i18n keys + tokens. **ELECTRIC FENCE (record-not-judge):** the SBAR fields capture what the OUTGOING NURSE WRITES — `assessment` is the nurse's OWN written assessment (a SBAR section, like a note's SOAP assessment), NOT a computed score; there is DELIBERATELY no acuity/severity/score/risk/priority/flag column, and NOTHING auto-populates any field (proven: a fresh stay has 0 handovers, and a recorded handover's assessment is verbatim; the payload carries no judgment key — recursive scan). NO AI-drafted handover this gate. **No charge posted (billing is G6).** No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED with the handover route — doctor 200 / billing 403); G1–G4 + reconciliation/fence/immutability/eval suites stay green. - `(pending)` HOSPITAL.G6: bed-to-billing — inpatient accrual + discharge invoice via the EXISTING billing engine (reconciles-to-the-unit). An inpatient stay accrues charges (bed-days + services) through the existing engine, and discharge produces an invoice that reconciles-to-the-unit. Built per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE1-ADT-MAP.md` §2.3/§5. **Opening:** top commit `ab9fc26` (HOSPITAL.G5), CI green. **KEY POSTURE — NET-NEW is STRICTLY ORCHESTRATION, zero new billing/pricing/VAT/line-total math:** the engine prices everything — a bed-day is a tenant-authored `TariffItem`, accrual is the existing `ChargeCaptureService::captureManual` (the engine resolves + **snapshots** the fee and computes `line_total`), and the discharge invoice is the existing `ChargeValidator::validateForPatientPeriod` → `IssueService::createDraftFromCharges` → `issue` flow (gapless number + PDF); reconciliation is the existing `ReconciliationEngine` (I4 "N charges → 1 invoice" natively). **`bed_day_accruals`** (BelongsToTenant) — the idempotency ledger `unique(tenant_id, stay_id, service_date)` (charge_id FK; NO money stored). **`BedBillingService`** (orchestration): `CATALOG_KEY='hospital'`; `STARTER` = 3 GENERIC bed-day items (BED-DAY-GENERAL 50000 / ICU 200000 / ISOLATION 80000 minor units — the tenant's OWN codes, **NO licensed code set**, placeholder rates it edits); `seedStarter` (gate `billing.manage`, idempotent by code, unit 'bed-day', vat 0); `accrueBedDays(actor, stay, ?upTo)` captures one bed-day Charge per occupied calendar day via the engine, idempotent via the ledger (fast-check then capture+ledger in one `DB::transaction`; a race rolls back on the unique key); `invoiceStay(actor, stay)` (gate `billing.manage`, cross-tenant fail-closed) accrues the final bed-days then runs the existing validate→draft→issue flow filtered to the stay window; the bed resolves via a typed `Bed::findOrFail` (fail-closed, no mis-price fallback). **`Console\AccrueBedDaysCommand`** (`hospital:accrue-bed-days`) — the unattended sweep, shaped exactly like `nursing:materialize-visits`: iterate ACTIVE tenants, resolve the tenant's `org_admin` as the automated billing actor (holds `billing.manage`; skip+warn if none), accrue every ADMITTED stay; registered in `HospitalServiceProvider`, scheduled `->dailyAt('05:30')->withoutOverlapping(30)->onOneServer()` (before the 06:00 dunning / 06:30 reconcile sweeps). **`BedBillingController::invoice`** (POST `/hospital/admissions/{stay}/invoice`, `billing.manage`, string-id FIX.1) → redirect to the EXISTING `billing.invoices.show`; light ADT wiring adds `actions.can_invoice`/`invoice_url` + an "Invoice stay" button on a discharged stay (`Admission.vue`, i18n key). **RECONCILES-TO-THE-UNIT (THE key proof):** a discharged stay's bed-day + service charges assemble into one gapless invoice and `ReconciliationEngine::check(period)` passes with **I4 `delta_minor === 0`**. **ELECTRIC FENCE / no-money-math (adversarial grep, tested):** `Modules\Hospital` contains none of `line_total_minor`/`vat_total_minor`/`subtotal_minor`/`vatMinor`/`intdiv(` — the only money it names is the authored per-diem RATE; all charge/VAT/line-total math lives in Billing. **One existing test updated by NECESSITY, not weakened:** `ScheduleRegistrationTest` (the canonical scheduled-command inventory — "guards against a future gate quietly scheduling something unattended") got the new sweep added to its expected cadence map + exact-set list, exactly as prior gates did for the nursing/billing sweeps; the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED with the invoice route (reception POST 403). Added tests: `BedBillingTest` (7 — per-diem tenant-authored [generic codes, integer minor units, zero VAT, no licensed set], accrue via the engine + snapshot, idempotent command [twice ≠ double-charge], **discharge invoice + reconcile δ=0** [6 bed-days × 50000 + 1 consult × 12000 = 312000; every invariant green], fee snapshot [editing the tariff never changes a past charge], no-money-math grep, RBAC + cross-tenant fail-closed). See [[D-118]].

The operational dispensing + stock domain, per the map §1. **Opening:** top commit `54735a4` (PHARMACY.G3), CI green. **(1) NET-NEW inventory:** `medication_stocks` (BelongsToTenant) is the MUTABLE current on-hand per formulary item (the Bed-status analogue) — `on_hand`, `unit`, `reorder_threshold` (a plain number); mutated ONLY under a FOR UPDATE row lock (`MedicationStock::lockOnHand` — the `BedService::lockBedStatus` idiom); `unique(tenant, formulary_item_id)`. `stock_movements` (BelongsToTenant, **APPEND-ONLY** — model guards + DB triggers `stock_movements_no_update`/`_no_delete`) is the immutable ledger (type received/dispensed/adjusted, signed `quantity_change`, `resulting_on_hand`, reason, soft `dispense_id`); the current on_hand stays consistent with the latest movement. `StockService` = `receive` (+qty, creates the stock row on first receipt) + `adjust` (stock-take to an absolute count, reason required) + `forTenant`/`recentMovements`, each locking then writing a movement (gate `dispense.manage`). **(2) DISPENSING:** `dispenses` (BelongsToTenant, **LogsReads**, **APPEND-ONLY**) records a pharmacist dispensing a quantity against a G2 order. `DispensingService::dispense` (gate **`dispense.manage`**; tenant + patient fail-closed) does a **factual state check** (the order must be active — can't dispense a discontinued order) then, in ONE transaction, **locks the stock FOR UPDATE → asserts on_hand ≥ qty (else `insufficientStock`) → creates the Dispense → decrements on_hand → appends the 'dispensed' movement** — ATOMIC + concurrency-safe. **(3) THE SAFE + CONCURRENCY-SAFE DECREMENT (the crux):** no oversell, no negative on-hand; proven by `DispenseParallelHammerTest` — 8 OS processes race to dispense the last unit (`pharmacy:attempt-dispense`, the `hospital:attempt-bed-claim` sibling), exactly ONE wins + 7 `INSUFFICIENT` + on_hand=0. **(4) FENCE (operational):** stock/dispensing are FACTS — "below stock" is a factual `on_hand <= reorder_threshold` comparison (`isBelowThreshold`), NEVER a graded/severity/alert judgment; **NO safety checking in dispensing** (the seam is orders/administration); no computed-judgment/safety column on any of the three tables (schema fence). **(5) RBAC:** dispensing/inventory **reuse `dispense.manage`** (the G1 pharmacist/pharmacy_technician permission — NO new permission); the per-patient dispensing view reads `patient.view`, read-logged. `InventoryController` (GET `/pharmacy/inventory`, `dispense.manage`) → `Inventory.vue` (stock + below-threshold factual + receive + adjust + movement log); `DispensingController` (GET `/pharmacy/patients/{patient}/dispensing`, `patient.view`, read-logged) → `Dispensing.vue` (active orders + on-hand + dispense + history) + i18n; audited via app-layer `Dispense::created` (`medication.dispensed`, patient) + `StockMovement::created` (`stock.<type>`, tenant) hooks so Pharmacy stays free of Audit. **No charge (pharmacy billing is G5).** No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED — inventory + dispensing GET 200 + reception dispense 403); all vertical + arch + eval + fence suites stay green. Added `DispensingTest` (6 — receive/adjust [append-only + on-hand consistent + audited]; dispense [decrements, atomic, tied to order, audited + read-logged]; the stock guard [no oversell + no negative]; discontinued-order refused [factual state check]; fence [below-threshold factual, no graded/safety column]; RBAC + tenant/patient fail-closed) + `DispenseParallelHammerTest` (1 — the concurrency proof: 1 of 8 wins the last unit, on_hand=0). VERIFIED: npm run build green; composer check FULLY green (Pint `passed` · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · **Pest 789 passed / 2 skipped / 6846 assertions**, 0 failed — +7 tests vs G3's 782); composer test:smoke green (3). **Next: G5 pharmacy billing** (a formulary item's TariffItem → captureManual → invoice → reconcile-to-the-unit, the bed-day precedent, no new math). See [[D-123]].
- `(pending)` PHARMACY.G5: pharmacy billing — a dispensed med accrues a charge through the EXISTING engine (reconciles-to-the-unit); **Phase 2 core COMPLETE**. The final buildable pharmacy gate, per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE2-PHARMACY-MAP.md` §2.1/§5. **Opening:** top commit `92f8c1a` (PHARMACY.G4), CI green. **KEY POSTURE — NET-NEW is STRICTLY ORCHESTRATION, zero new billing/pricing/VAT/line-total math** (the bed-day HOSPITAL.G6 pattern, mirrored). **(1) MEDICATION PRICING — a formulary item maps to a tenant-authored `TariffItem`:** a soft nullable `tariff_item_id` on `formulary_items` (no FK — Pharmacy stays decoupled, the `stay_id` shape); `PharmacyBillingService::priceItem` (gate `billing.manage`, tenant fail-closed) `updateOrCreate`s a `TariffItem` in a get-or-created `pharmacy` `TariffCatalog` (the tenant's OWN `MED-*` code, integer minor units, zero VAT, `requires_service_documentation=false`) and links it — pricing lives in the EXISTING billing/tariff store, **NOT duplicated in pharmacy**, and **NO licensed drug pricing bundled** (the tenant authors placeholder rates it edits, the `BedBillingService::seedStarter` discipline). **(2) CHARGE ON DISPENSE via the EXISTING engine:** `chargeForDispense(actor, Dispense)` → `ChargeCaptureService::captureManual($patient, $branch, $dispensed_at, $item->code, $quantity, $actor)` — the engine resolves the tariff by code, **SNAPSHOTS the fee**, and computes `line_total` (qty × price); Pharmacy does NO money math. IDEMPOTENT via a new `dispense_charges` link `unique(tenant, dispense_id)` (a dispense is charged once; re-charging returns the same Charge; NO money stored — the Charge is the money). An UNPRICED med returns null (no charge, no gate) so the G4 dispensing tests stay green. Wired **BEST-EFFORT + DECOUPLED** in `DispensingController` AFTER the dispense commits (`try { … } catch (Throwable)`) — a billing hiccup NEVER blocks the concurrency-critical dispense (reconcilable later). **(3) INVOICE + RECONCILE-TO-THE-UNIT (THE key proof):** `invoicePatient(actor, patient, from, to)` runs the EXISTING `validateForPatientPeriod` → `createDraftFromCharges(SELF_PAY)` → `issue` flow (gapless number + PDF) over the patient's validated, uninvoiced charges in the window; a patient invoice INCLUDING a dispensed-med charge assembles into one gapless invoice and `ReconciliationEngine::check(period)` passes with **I4 `delta_minor === 0`** (for INPATIENT, pharmacy charges join the stay's discharge invoice via the existing G6 `invoiceStay` — same gather-by-patient+period, no new invoice logic). **(4) RBAC:** the `pharmacist` role gains the EXISTING `billing.manage` — RbacProvisioner const + a `provisionTenant`-all backfill migration; **NO new permission** (billing.manage already exists), so `RbacTest`'s permission-count stays green; new tenants via the Tenant `created` hook. A `PricingController` (GET `/pharmacy/pricing` + POST `/pharmacy/pricing/{item}`, `billing.manage`, string-id FIX.1) → `Pharmacy/Pricing.vue` (set a med's price like any tariff item) + i18n. **(5) ELECTRIC FENCE / no-money-math (adversarial grep, tested):** a med price is a RATE (financial), never a safety/appropriateness/substitution verdict — no cost-based substitution suggestion (substitution/safety is the certified-partner seam, not billing); `Modules\Pharmacy` contains none of `line_total_minor`/`vat_total_minor`/`subtotal_minor`/`vatMinor`/`intdiv(` (the only money it names is the authored per-unit RATE; all charge/VAT/line-total math lives in Billing); no cost/clinical-judgment column on `formulary_items`. No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED — pricing GET 200 + reception price-set 403); all vertical + arch + eval + fence suites stay green. Added `PharmacyBillingTest` (6 — med priced as a tenant-authored TariffItem [own code, integer minor units, zero VAT, no licensed pricing]; dispense captures a Charge via the engine + snapshot + idempotent [twice ≠ double-charge]; **patient invoice WITH a med charge reconciles δ=0** [2 × 800 = 1600, I4 green]; no-money-math grep + no-judgment-column fence; fee snapshot [re-pricing never changes a past charge]; RBAC billing.manage + cross-tenant fail-closed). VERIFIED: npm run build green; composer check FULLY green (Pint `passed` · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · **Pest 795 passed / 2 skipped / 7056 assertions**, 0 failed — +6 tests vs G4's 789); composer test:smoke green (3). **PHARMACY CORE COMPLETE: G1 formulary + safety-seam → G2 orders → G3 eMAR → G4 dispensing/inventory → G5 billing.** The ONE deliberate gap is the medication-safety JUDGMENT — an EMPTY `NullMedicationSafetyProvider` seam invoked-but-no-op throughout (G2 placement, G3 administration), the certified-partner binding point; a homemade checker is a PERMANENT non-goal (medical-device territory). Phases 3–7 (lab / radiology / OR / ED) remain. See [[D-124]].
- `(pending)` PHARMACY.G5-FIX: stabilize a PRE-EXISTING `PortalUiTest` date/time flake surfaced by G5's CI run (NOT a G5 regression — the follow-up to `a49b803`). The G5 commit's `composer check` was FULLY green locally, but CI (MySQL 8, run at 21:46 UTC) went RED on `portal cancellation enforces the cancel window policy server-side` with `BookingUnavailableException::outsideAvailability` — thrown during the test's SETUP, not any pharmacy code (my diff touches nothing in Scheduling/Portal/Booking; the test last changed at `b91e2d3`). **Root cause:** the test books `$soon = now()->addHours(2)` with a 30-minute service against a resource available `00:00–23:59`; when real `now()` is ~21:30–21:59 the slot starts ~23:46 and its 30-min end crosses midnight past the 23:59 window → `outsideAvailability`. A ~30-min DAILY flake window that only bites when CI happens to run in it (local passed at a different time-of-day) — the canonical "local green ≠ CI green" (see [[verify-ci-directly-github-api]]). **Fix (test-only, deterministic):** `Carbon::setTestNow('2026-06-15 09:00:00')` at the top of the test so `now()+2h`/`now()+10d` are stable in-hours, + a file-level `afterEach(fn () => Carbon::setTestNow())` (a no-op for tests that never freeze) so a frozen instant can never leak forward even on a mid-test failure. No production code, no new test, no count change (the test already passed locally: still Pest 795 / 2-skip / 7056 local; on CI's Redis the 2 Horizon skips run → 797 passed). Verified: `PortalUiTest` 9/9 green (deterministic); Pint `passed`; full composer check re-run green; CI re-run green. See [[D-124]], [[verify-ci-directly-github-api]].

STRICTLY ORCHESTRATION — NO new billing/pricing/VAT/line-total math (the surgery-G5 / bed-day-HOSPITAL.G6 shape, mirrored). **(1) TENANT-AUTHORED PRICING:** an `ed` `TariffCatalog` holds each billable as a `TariffItem` (integer minor units, `vat_rate_bp` 0, NO licensed pricing) — the ED attendance/facility fee (`priceAttendance`, fixed `ED-ATTENDANCE` code) + each ED service (`priceService`, own code); via `authorTariff` = `updateOrCreate(catalog,code)`. **(2) CHARGE CAPTURE via the EXISTING `ChargeCaptureService::captureManual`:** `chargeVisit(actor, visit, attendance, serviceCodes[])` (gate `billing.manage`, tenant fail-closed) pushes a capture per billable (attendance ×1 + each selected service ×1); the ENGINE resolves the tariff by code, SNAPSHOTS the fee, computes the line total — ED does NO money math. Service_date = `dispositioned_at ?? arrived_at`. IDEMPOTENT via `ed_visit_charges` (`unique(tenant,charge_id)`; stores NO money). **(3) RECONCILES-TO-THE-UNIT (THE key proofs):** (a) a DISCHARGED patient's `invoiceVisit` (`validateForPatientPeriod` → gather VALIDATED uninvoiced charges on the service day → `createDraftFromCharges(SELF_PAY)` → `issue`) → `ReconciliationEngine::check` passes + **I4 δ=0** with ED charges present; (b) THE COMPOSITE EPISODE — an ADMITTED patient's ED charges are patient charges with a service_date in the stay window, so Hospital's `BedBillingService::invoiceStay` sweeps them onto the stay's discharge invoice ALONGSIDE the bed-days (ED never imports Hospital — the shared engine) → **I4 δ=0** with ED-attendance + BED-DAY-GENERAL on ONE invoice (the whole emergency→inpatient episode bills as one reconciling invoice). Both proven (clock frozen for a deterministic period). **(4) RBAC:** pricing/charge/invoice reuse `billing.manage` (NO new permission — the billing office bills, NOT the ED team; an `ed_physician` [ed.manage, no billing.manage] + reception are REFUSED). **(5) FENCE (financial):** a price is a RATE, never a clinical/appropriateness verdict — the ED-attendance fee is a plain `TariffItem`, **NOT acuity-driven** (proven: two visits triaged ESI 1 vs ESI 5 get the SAME 2000 fee — the acuity is a clinical record, the fee is a tariff, kept separate); fees snapshotted (a later re-price never changes a past charge); the money-math grep over `Modules\ED\src` + the app-layer composer is CLEAN (`line_total_minor`/`vat_total_minor`/`subtotal_minor`/`vatMinor`/`intdiv(` — zero); `ed_visit_charges` carries no money/acuity/severity column. **(6) UI (P0D.GU):** `EdBillingController` (`/ed/visits/{visit}/billing`, string-id FIX.1 — pricing surface + capture + invoice; an admitted visit shows "joins the stay invoice" instead of a visit-level invoice) + `ED/Billing.vue`; the disposition page links to it (billing.manage-gated); `ed.billing.*` i18n. FIX.5 smoke EXTENDED (`/ed/visits/{id}/billing` GET 200; reception charge 403). No audit hook for the link table (Charge/Invoice audited by Billing — the `DispenseCharge`/`SurgicalCaseCharge` precedent). No existing behavior test modified; **the reconciliation/fence/immutability/clinical-safety/triage-eval + G1–G5 + Phase-1/2/5 suites stay green** (the engine is REUSED, not changed). Added `tests/Feature/ED/EdBillingTest.php` (7). VERIFIED locally: ED billing 7 + smoke 3 passed; npm build green; PHPStan L5 on ED + app-layer `[OK]`; Pint passed; money-math grep clean. composer check + CI verified after push. **PHASE 6 (EMERGENCY DEPARTMENT) COMPLETE** — G1→G6: an ED runs end-to-end (arrival/EdVisit → triage[nurse-assigned acuity] → tracking board → clinical documentation[reuse Clinical] → disposition + the ED→ADT handoff[admit reuses AdmissionService → inpatient Stay] → billing[reconciles-to-the-unit, incl. the composite emergency→inpatient episode on one invoice]). Deliberate seam: COMPUTED triage acuity = certified-partner / permanent non-goal (the empty `TriageAcuityProvider`). Remaining hospital phases: Lab (Phase 3) + Radiology (Phase 4) — mostly integration shells pending HL7/FHIR + PACS/DICOM partners. See [[D-135]].

- `(pending)` APPROVAL.P4: Approval Queue edit-before-sending — wire the wireframe's [Edit before sending] over the EXISTING `editedPayload` approve support. Fourth fix part of the APPROVAL-QUEUE chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/APPROVAL-QUEUE-DIFF.md` §8 item 2 (now **RESOLVED**). **Purely additive over the existing support — NO second approve path, NO new backend judgment; an edit is NOT a bypass.** **Opening:** top commit `cb79b27` (docs reconciliation on `aac95c8` APPROVAL.P3), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder (2 real pending actions + a seeded demo.echo for the edit demo). **Step 1 (confirmed, read-only):** `ApprovalQueue::approve($action,$reviewer,?$editedPayload)` (`Modules/AiCore/src/Services/ApprovalQueue.php`) ALREADY accepted an edited payload: it re-authorises the reviewer against `$tool->definition()->permission`, `assertPending`, uses `$payload = $editedPayload ?? $action->input_payload`, re-runs `$tool->execute($payload)` (which re-grounds/re-derives from live state), and records the `edited_payload` column. The live controller was approving AS-IS (no edited payload) and the UI had no editor. **(1) Service provenance (extend, not judge):** `approve()` now stamps human-edited when `$editedPayload !== null` — `result['human_edited']=true` on the executed result (beside the tool's own provenance, e.g. DraftReplyTool's `ai_assisted`), and `metadata:['human_edited'=>true]` on the `approved` + `executed` `ai_interactions` rows. The `edited_payload` column already distinguished edited from unedited; this adds the marker to the posted result + the append-only ledger. Unedited approve = null editedPayload → NONE of these (unchanged). **(2) Controller** (`app/Http/Controllers/AiApprovalQueueController.php`): `approve()` reads an OPTIONAL `edited_payload` (`validate(['edited_payload'=>'sometimes|array'])` — still can't raise autonomy or swap the tool) and passes it to `ApprovalQueue::approve`; flash `approved_edited` when edited. `presentPending` surfaces a new `inputPayload` (the tool INPUT execute re-grounds — distinct from the read-only `proposed_output` preview). **(3) UI** (`resources/js/pages/Governance/ApprovalQueue.vue`): an **Edit before sending** pill (rendered only when `canReview`) opens an inline editor seeded with `inputPayload` (JSON); **Approve edited** parses it (invalid JSON refused client-side) and POSTs `{edited_payload}` to the SAME approve route; reuses the P1/P2 visual + the P3 approve-contract caption (applies to the edited approve too). **(4) i18n:** unique `aiQueue.edit.*` sub-block + `flash.approved_edited` (en). **THE GATE (unchanged — the edit changes CONTENT, not the gate):** the edited approve STILL re-authorises + re-grounds + asserts-pending — proven by 5 new tests. **RBAC** canReview-gated (server re-authorises regardless — no weaker path). **NO existing behavior test modified; the electric-fence eval + AiApprovalQueue/anatomy/caption/chrome suites stay green.** Added `tests/Feature/Governance/ApprovalEditBeforeSendingTest.php` (5 tests / ~35 assertions: an edited approve posts the EDITED payload [demo.echo `result.message` = the edit] + records human-edited [result + ledger metadata]; an UNEDITED approve carries no human_edited marker [distinguishable]; an edited approve STILL re-authorises [ai.manage-only reviewer on a scheduler.suggest_slots action → 403, nothing runs]; STILL asserts still-pending [editing an executed action → AiCoreException, result not overwritten]; an edited CLINICAL draft [clinical.summarize_since_last_visit / note.write] still re-authorised → a reviewer without note.write denied [edit does not lower the clinical bar]). BROWSER-VERIFIED (Praxis Lindenhof, org_admin): edit a demo.echo draft → "Approve edited" → flash "…posted through the same gate — recorded as human-edited."; DB confirms `result.message`='EDITED by the reviewer in the browser', `result.human_edited`=true, `edited_payload` recorded, ledger executed row `metadata.human_edited`=true; Reject still works ("Action rejected. Nothing was executed."). VERIFIED locally: `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors`, **Pest 1009 passed** (13948 assertions); `composer test:smoke` green (3); `npm run build` green (ApprovalQueue chunk). composer check + CI verified after push.
