- `(pending)` APPROVAL.P5: Approval Queue — model fence-refused as a countable outcome + the governance stat strip from REAL data (no faked numbers). Fifth fix part of the APPROVAL-QUEUE chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/APPROVAL-QUEUE-DIFF.md` §8 items 4 (fence-refused modelling) + 3 (stat strip) — both now **RESOLVED**. **The fence is UNCHANGED — only the RECORDING is new; every stat is REAL or honestly absent.** **Opening:** top commit `19d39c4` (APPROVAL.P4), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder (2 real pending actions incl. a handoff draft). **Step 1 (confirmed):** the fence refuses at approve time in `DraftReplyTool::execute` — a handed-off/empty draft throws (`preview()` re-grounds → handoff:true) — but that refusal was NOT a countable outcome: the controller caught the `AiCoreException` and left the action PENDING; the only pre-draft fence (InboxAgent clinical refusal) writes an `ai_interactions` `refused` row but creates no AgentAction. So a fence refusal at the queue was not queryable/listable. **(1) The refusal, RECORDED (not the fence changed):** new `Modules\AiCore\Exceptions\FenceRefusalException` extends `AiCoreException` (so every existing `catch (AiCoreException)` still catches it); `DraftReplyTool::execute` now throws IT (same condition, same message, same moment). `ApprovalQueue::approve()` wraps `tool->execute()` in `catch (FenceRefusalException)` → `recordFenceRefusal()`: transitions the action to the new `AgentAction::STATUS_FENCE_REFUSED` (+ `fence_refused_at`; the fence's OWN reason kept in `rejection_reason`, distinguished from a human reject by the status), writes an **append-only `fence_refused` `ai_interactions` row** (metadata.reason), fires `AgentActionLifecycleChanged($action,'fence_refused')` → the EXISTING generic glue audits `agent_action.fence_refused` (no glue change) → re-throws. Nothing executes. Migration `2026_08_22_000001` adds ONLY `fence_refused_at` (the `status` column is a plain string — the new value needs no schema change). **CONFIRMED the fence's behavior/when-it-fires is UNCHANGED:** a non-handoff action still executes; the electric-fence eval (`tests/Evals/`, 37/398) is untouched + green; inbox/live agent suites green (the exception-type change is transparent — it's still an AiCoreException). **(2) The stat strip — REAL data only** (`AiApprovalQueueController::index` passes `stats`): **pending** = real pending count; **fenceRefused** = real `fence_refused` count (danger-tint per wireframe); **approvedPct (30d)** = executed ÷ resolved-in-window (executed+rejected+fence_refused) from real timestamps; **avgReviewMinutes (30d)** = avg(resolvedAt−createdAt) from real timestamps. **THE HONESTY RULE:** approvedPct + avgReviewMinutes are computed ONLY when there is a real denominator — with **no resolved action in the window they are `null` → the UI renders an honest "—"**, never 0/estimate. So pending + fence count are always real; approved-% + avg-review are real when resolved records exist, honestly absent otherwise. Controller: `approve()` catches `FenceRefusalException` FIRST (before AiCoreException, subclass ordering) → redirect with status `fence_refused` (honest flash, not the reviewer's error); resolved query + `presentResolved` now include `fence_refused` (a new `resolvedAt()` helper coalesces executed/rejected/fence timestamps). **(3) UI** (`resources/js/pages/Governance/ApprovalQueue.vue`): a 4-tile stat strip (Pending / Approved·30d / Avg review·30d / Refused-by-fence[danger]) with `orDash()` for null metrics; a warning-tint `fence_refused` flash; the Resolved badge gained the `fence_refused` (warning) variant via `resolvedStatusClass()`. **(4) i18n** unique `aiQueue.stats.*` sub-block + `flash.fence_refused` + `status.fence_refused` (en). **NO existing behavior test modified.** Added `tests/Feature/Governance/ApprovalFenceRefusedTest.php` (4 tests / 55 assertions: a real handoff draft [Comms Thread + non-groundable patient message] approved → recorded fence_refused [status + fence_refused_at + reason + append-only ledger + audit], nothing executes, no staff message; only the fence path records fence_refused [a clean demo.echo still executes — trigger unchanged]; the stat strip shows real pending + fence 0 + honest null approvedPct/avgReview when nothing resolved; the fence count=1 + approvedPct=50 + avgReview≠null computed from real resolved records via the controller approve). BROWSER-VERIFIED (Praxis Lindenhof, org_admin): approving the seeded handoff draft → flash "The electric fence refused this action…recorded as fence-refused", the Refused-by-fence tile → 1, it appears in Resolved as Fence-refused; pending/approved-%/avg-review show real values. **CAUGHT (the lying-exit-code memory):** the first `composer check` reported exit 0 but Pint had FAILED (FenceRefusalException empty-body + a test unused import) — re-ran Pint --test manually, auto-fixed, re-ran the full check. VERIFIED locally: `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK]`, **Pest 1013 passed**; `composer test:smoke` green; `npm run build` green. composer check + CI verified after push. Remaining APPROVAL parts: bulk-approve (excludes clinical/financial), resolved search/filters/reviewer/grouping. See [[AiCore]].
- `(pending)` APPROVAL.P6: Approval Queue — the Resolved view: search + status/date/reviewer filters + grouping, over REAL resolved data. Sixth fix part of the APPROVAL-QUEUE chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/APPROVAL-QUEUE-DIFF.md` §8 item 6 (now **RESOLVED**). **Every filter/search/count maps to a REAL field; no fabricated entry/attribute; tenant + RBAC scoped.** **Opening:** top commit `2b51150` (APPROVAL.P5), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **(1) Controller** (`app/Http/Controllers/AiApprovalQueueController.php` `index` + new helpers): the resolved section is now server-side searchable/filterable over the REAL resolved set (executed/rejected/P5 fence_refused). **`allowedToolKeys($tools,$actor)`** = the tool keys whose declared permission the actor holds (the SAME per-tool gate approve enforces) → the base resolved query `whereIn('tool_key', $allowed)` **RBAC-scopes** the list AND the counts (a reviewer never sees/counts actions they couldn't act on; unregistered key excluded fail-closed); tenant-scoped by `BelongsToTenant`. **`resolvedFilters($request)`** reads real query params (rstatus∈all/executed/rejected/fence_refused; rq; rreviewer; rfrom/rto — dates regex-guarded). **`applyResolvedFilters`** applies search+reviewer+date (NOT the status pill): the free-text search matches `tool_key`/`agent`/`feature`/`why`/`rejection_reason` (real text columns — NOT the nested payload, stated honestly); reviewer→`reviewed_by`; date→`COALESCE(executed_at,rejected_at,fence_refused_at)` (portable). **Real per-status counts** via `selectRaw('status,count(*)')->groupBy('status')` over the filtered base (before the status pill). List ordered by the COALESCE resolved timestamp DESC, limit 20. `presentResolved` enriched: `toolName` (real declared name), **`reviewerName`** (bulk-resolved from `users.name`), **`systemAttributed`** (true for fence_refused → the fence, NOT the human who clicked; reviewerName null), `rejectionReason` (reject reason OR fence reason). `resolvedReviewers` = distinct real human reviewers (executed/rejected, RBAC-allowed) for the dropdown. **(2) UI** (`resources/js/pages/Governance/ApprovalQueue.vue`): the Resolved view (behind the P1 toggle) gained a "Search resolved…" box (debounced 300ms), status filter pills with real counts (All/Approved/Rejected/Fence-refused[danger dot]), a reviewer `<select>` + from/to date inputs + a Clear; results **grouped by day** (client-side over the returned rows — presentational), each row: tool name·agent, attribution ("Resolved by {reviewer}·{time}" or "Refused by the electric fence (system)·{time}"), the real reason in quotes, and the outcome badge (`resolvedStatusClass`). Filters push via `router.get('/governance/approvals', params, {preserveState, only:['resolved','resolvedCounts','resolvedReviewers','resolvedFilters']})`. **(3) i18n** extended the unique `aiQueue.resolved.*` sub-block (search/filterAll/filterApproved/filterRejected/filterFenceRefused with {count}, anyReviewer, dateFrom/dateTo, clear, noneForFilters, byReviewer, bySystem). **THE HONESTY:** every row + count is a real record; the Fence-refused pill uses the P5 `fence_refused` status (system-attributed + fence reason); no fabricated attribute is filterable. **NO existing behavior test modified; the electric-fence eval + AiApprovalQueue + all suites stay green.** Added `tests/Feature/Governance/ApprovalResolvedViewTest.php` (4 tests / 110 assertions: real outcome badge + reviewer/system attribution + real reason + real counts; the Fence-refused filter returns only the system-attributed fence rows with the fence reason; search matches the real `why` column + the reviewer filter maps to `reviewed_by`; tenant + RBAC scoping — an ai.manage-only reviewer sees the demo.echo but NOT the comms.draft_reply fence refusal, and counts reflect only permitted, a second tenant sees none). BROWSER-VERIFIED (Praxis Lindenhof, org_admin): seeded an approved/executed + rejected-with-reason + fence_refused; the Resolved view groups by day with correct badges + reviewer/system attribution + reasons; search filters by a real field; the Approved/Rejected/Fence-refused pills filter by real status with real counts; the reviewer + date sub-filters work. **CAUGHT the lying-exit-code again earlier in the chain — always re-run Pint --test.** VERIFIED locally: `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK]`, **Pest 1017 passed**; `composer test:smoke` green; `npm run build` green (ApprovalQueue chunk). composer check + CI verified after push. Third fix part of the BRANCHES chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/BRANCHES-DIFF.md` §6/item 8 (now **RESOLVED**). **A FIX (stop an invalid edit), NOT a trim (no functionality removed).** **Opening:** top commit `1a9ac1e` (BRANCH.P2), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **The model (what distinguishes a practitioner):** `Resource.type` ∈ practitioner/room/chair/vehicle; a **practitioner** is PERSON-BACKED — it carries a `staff_profile_id` (→ `StaffProfile`), and the model invariant `assertValidResource()` lets ONLY a practitioner link a staff profile. Facility resources (room/chair/vehicle) are free-standing. Practitioner resources are provisioned via People (staff), NOT admin-created here (`ResourceController::CREATABLE_TYPES` = room/chair/vehicle). **THE BUG:** `ResourceController::update` ran `validateResource` (facility-only `in:`) + passed `type` to update for ALL resources — so a practitioner's type would be forced toward a facility type (a room/chair/vehicle select can't even represent a practitioner). **THE FIX:** `update` now branches — for a **practitioner** it validates the NAME only and IGNORES any submitted `type` (the practitioner stays a practitioner; a forged `type=room` has no effect); for a **facility** it keeps name+type editable within the facility-only `in:room,chair,vehicle` rule (so a facility also can't become a practitioner → 422). UI (`Admin/Branches.vue`): a practitioner row renders a read-only "Practitioner" label (`resources.type.practitioner` i18n) instead of the room/chair/vehicle `<select>`; facility rows keep the editable select. **A FIX not a trim:** practitioner name + status (activate/deactivate, still guarded) stay editable; facility-resource CRUD is UNCHANGED; only the invalid type-edit is stopped. server-enforced + tenant-scoped + admin.manage-gated. **NO existing behavior test modified; W8c ResourceCrud + P1/P2 + electric-fence eval + all suites stay green; correctly-more-real (practitioner rows in the roster) un-regressed — kept, just shown with their true non-editable type.** Added `tests/Feature/Scheduling/BranchPractitionerResourceTest.php` (6 tests / 30 assertions: the payload exposes type=practitioner [so the UI renders read-only] + resourceTypes stays facility-only; a forged retype of a practitioner→room is IGNORED [stays practitioner, name still updates]; a facility keeps its editable type [room→chair works]; a facility→practitioner retype is refused [422]; practitioner status stays editable [deactivate/activate work]; RBAC admin.manage [reception 403] + tenant-scoped [cross-tenant 404]). NOTE: the test practitioner has staff_profile_id=null on purpose — it proves the CONTROLLER-level fix works on its own (not relying on the model's staff-profile guard). the per-tool SETTINGS.P2 cap is unchanged and is one of the min() terms.** **NO UI this gate** (P2+ builds the surfaces; RBAC admin.manage is applied by the P2 config controller — no route yet). **NO existing behavior test modified; FINAL fix part of the AGENT-TOOL-CONFIG chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/AGENT-TOOL-CONFIG-DIFF.md` §9 items 5/5b/6 (now ALL **RESOLVED**). **THE RULES: limits are REAL settings the runtime reads (or honestly deferred — never phantom); the uncertainty-escalation is ALWAYS-ON (no disable path, floor un-removable); a new agent is a P1 governed container capped from birth.** **Opening:** top commit `4239ca9` (AGENT.P5), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **(1) RATE & TIMING LIMITS (REAL — the runtime CONSULTS them):** additive migration `2026_08_26_000001` adds `max_drafts_per_hour` (nullable) + `quiet_hours_start`/`quiet_hours_end` (nullable, hour-of-day 0-23) to `agents`. `AgentRuntime::runTool` (on the Agent-entity path, after the OFF check, before AUTO/pending) now CONSULTS them: quiet hours (`Agent::isQuietHour(now-hour)`, handles overnight wrap) → records + returns `quiet_hours` (deferred to a human); max drafts/hour → counts the agent-KIND's AgentActions in the rolling hour (`whereIn agent`, `AgentRegistry::ledgerNames($agent->kind())`, `created_at>=now-1h`) and at the cap → records + returns `rate_limited`. A limit can only STOP the agent, never widen it. Stored per-agent via `AgentConfigService::configure` (new `applyLimit` — validated bounds [max 1..1000, hours 0..23], nullable clears, non-numeric ignored; audited). First fix part of the BILLING-AR chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/BILLING-AR-DIFF.md` §4 gap #6 (now **RESOLVED** — the money-integrity foundation; unblocks the P2 roll-forward). **The roll-forward couldn't tie out because write-offs/adjustments were unmodeled; this gate models them as REAL append-only ledger movements that reconcile-to-the-unit, operator-gated, audited. Append-only: ORM guard (updating/deleting throw) + DB triggers (SIGNAL 45000 no_update/no_delete) + a non-zero CHECK — mirroring `payment_allocations`. Model `Modules\Billing\Models\InvoiceAdjustment` (BelongsToTenant, isReversal(), assertTenantReferences). **OPERATOR-GATED service** `Modules\Billing\Services\AdjustmentService::writeOff/contractualAdjustment/reverse` — all gate `billing.manage` (the money-commit gate; the AGENT has NO path — no AI tool calls it, so it can never write one, the fence), assert actor+invoice tenant, reason required, positive amount, `DB::transaction` locking the `invoice_balances` row (FOR UPDATE), an **over-adjustment guard** (`amountMinor > openBalance` → refused — can't reduce below zero, mirroring over-allocation), create the row, refresh the projection, audit (`billing.written_off`/`billing.adjusted`/`billing.adjustment_reversed`). A correction is a reversal row (re-reads the canonical persisted amount, never a caller-mutated instance; not-already-reversed + not-a-reversal guards). **(3) THE RECONCILE PROOF:** `PaymentService::openBalance` now = `total − net allocations − net adjustments` (added `netAllocatedMinor`/`netAdjustedMinor`); `refreshInvoiceBalance` (made public for the AdjustmentService) writes `open_balance_minor` = that, with the PAID/PARTIALLY_PAID status kept **payment-driven** (a write-off reduces what's owed without labelling it "paid"; identical to before for payment-only invoices). The **ReconciliationEngine extended, count unchanged at 6, tie-out NOT weakened**: **I2** derivation subtracts net adjustments (still 0≤open≤total); **I6** gains orphan-adjustment + adjustment-reversal-integrity checks (mirroring allocations). Because aging/outstanding read the reconciled `invoice_balances` projection, write-offs/adjustments flow into them automatically (no reporting change). **HONESTY/FENCE:** real integer-minor ledger movements; balance = charges − payments − credits − adjustments − write-offs; δ_minor === 0 WITH them; operator-gated (agent-never); append-only; NO page-side money math (no reporting this gate). **NOTHING weakened:** the reconcile-to-the-unit invariant, the allocation guard, the append-only ledger, the frozen invoices — all intact; the existing billing/reconcile/hammer suites pass untouched. **NO existing behavior test modified.** Added `tests/Feature/Billing/WriteOffAdjustmentTest.php` (7 tests / 85 assertions): a write-off posts an append-only movement (integer minor, reason, operator) + reduces the balance + reconciles (I1-I6, count 6, every δ=0) + audited; a contractual adjustment posts (with agreement ref) + reconciles; **THE RECONCILE PROOF — balance = charges − payments − adjustments − write-offs (20000−4000−3000−5000=8000) + reconciles δ=0**; operator-gated (a reception/non-billing.manage user refused on both; nothing posted); the over-adjustment guard (can't write off > open); append-only (the row can't update/delete → LogicException; a correction is a reversal row restoring the balance + reconciling; reversal-of-reversal + double-reversal refused); the base case unweakened (a payment-only invoice still δ=0). VERIFIED locally: full **billing suite green (93 tests / 765 assertions** incl. SimulatedMonth count-6/δ=0 + Reconciliation I1-I6 + the 7 new); `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK]`, **Pest 1100 passed**; `composer test:smoke` green. (No FE touched → no npm build.) composer check + CI verified after push. - `(pending)` BILLAR.P2: Billing/Reporting — the AR roll-forward (engine-computed reconcile-to-the-unit bridge; opening→closing ties out δ=0 over P1 movements). Second fix part of the BILLING-AR chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/BILLING-AR-DIFF.md` §4 item #1 (now **RESOLVED**; unblocked by P1's write-off/adjustment ledger). **The roll-forward is a MetricsService (engine) method that TIES OUT to the unit; the page (P6) will DISPLAY the six figures, never compute them. NO visual grid this gate.** **Opening:** top commit `b8994ef` (BILLAR.P1), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **Studied (confirmed):** `MetricsService` financial aggregates run over the reconciled `invoice_balances` projection (integer minor units, billing.view, tenant-scoped, definitions reused from the ReconciliationEngine so reporting agrees with billing); `invoicedTotalMinor` = Σ issued INV totals in range; `paymentsReceivedTotalMinor` = Σ gross payments (cash) — NOT what reduces AR; `outstandingBalanceMinor` = Σ open_balance_minor (point-in-time projection); P1 `invoice_adjustments` (signed minor, type write_off|contractual). Critical model detail: a full credit note fully cancels an unpaid ISSUED invoice, but the cancellation status (CANCELLED_BY_CREDIT_NOTE, open 0) lives on the `invoice_balances` PROJECTION — the frozen `invoices.status` stays ISSUED; partial credit notes do NOT reduce the invoice balance. **(1) The method** `MetricsService::arRollForward($actor, $from, $to): array` returns six integer-minor figures + the tie fields: **OPENING** = `outstandingAsOfMinor(from−1)` — AR derived from the append-only ledger AS-OF a date (Σ over issued INV of `total − allocations(allocated_at≤D) − adjustments(adjusted_at≤D)`, an invoice fully cancelled by a credit note issued ≤D counting 0); **CHARGES** = new issued INV totals in [from,to] **NET of credit-note cancellations in the period** (the fold that makes the bridge exhaustive — a cancelling CN removes the invoice's full total from AR; identified via the `invoice_balances` status, not the frozen invoice status, so partial CNs are correctly excluded); **COLLECTIONS** = net `PaymentAllocation`s (allocated_at in period — the movement that reduces an invoice's open balance, not gross cash); **ADJUSTMENTS** = P1 InvoiceAdjustment type=contractual in period; **WRITE-OFFS** = P1 InvoiceAdjustment type=write_off in period; **CLOSING** = `outstandingBalanceMinor()` (the reconciled projection, outstanding as of now/`to`), computed INDEPENDENTLY of the bridge (the current-period management view, "as of today"). **(2) THE TIE-OUT:** `bridge_closing_minor` = opening + charges − collections − adjustments − write_offs; `discrepancy_minor` = bridge − closing; `ties` = (discrepancy===0). The bridge (sum of period movements) MUST equal the independent reconciled projection, EXACTLY — δ=0 (telescoping: opening[as-of from−1] + period movements = as-of-`to` = the projection by I2; the credit-note fold covers the CN movement). **A non-tie is SURFACED, never papered over** (`ties`/`discrepancy_minor` exposed) — a non-tie means an unmodeled movement changed AR or the projection drifted (a reconcile failure to expose). Every AR movement in the period is one of the five modeled terms (charges [incl. credit notes], payments, P1 adjustments, P1 write-offs). **FENCE:** engine-computed + tying; the page (P6) will DISPLAY the six figures; NO page-side arithmetic (no JS bridge, no client sum); NO visual grid this gate. **NOTHING weakened:** the ReconciliationEngine + P1 + the allocation guard + the append-only ledger + I2 all intact; the roll-forward READS the reconciled projection + the append-only movements, writes nothing. billing.view + tenant-scoped (fail-closed via BelongsToTenant). **NO existing behavior test modified.** Added `tests/Feature/Billing/ArRollForwardTest.php` (7 tests / 43 assertions): the roll-forward returns the six integer-minor figures + ties (a fresh charge: opening 0/charges 20000/closing 20000/δ=0); **THE TIE-OUT — a period with a payment + a P1 contractual adjustment + a P1 write-off still ties** (0+20000−4000−3000−5000=8000, closing=8000, δ=0, and closing === the independent `outstandingBalanceMinor` projection); a payments-only period ties (base case); **OPENING AR carries a prior-period invoice** (issued before `from`) and the period ties (opening 15000, collections 5000, closing 10000); **a credit note in the period folds into charges (net) and still ties** (gross 10000 − credit 10000 = 0 charges, closing 0); **an unmodeled movement is SURFACED as a non-tie** (a raw `invoice_balances` projection mutation → bridge 20000 vs closing 15000 → δ=5000, ties=false — never hidden); billing.view-gated (reception refused) + tenant-scoped (a second tenant sees all zeros). VERIFIED locally: reporting + billing suites green (110 tests / 899 assertions); `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK]`, **Pest 1107 passed**; `composer test:smoke` green. Third fix part of the BILLING-AR chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/BILLING-AR-DIFF.md` §4 items #2/#3 (now **RESOLVED**). **Two engine metrics computed IN THE ENGINE (MetricsService) from real figures; the page (P6) DISPLAYS them, never page-computes. Returns the real components (`ar_minor`, `credit_sales_minor`, `days`) + `dso` (float rounded to **1 decimal** — money stays exact integer minor units; only the ratio rounds, documented). **Zero-sales period → `dso: null` ("—", NO divide-by-zero, not 0/∞).** **(2) NET COLLECTION RATE** `MetricsService::netCollectionRate($actor,$from,$to): array` — **rate = collections ÷ collectible**; **collectible = charges billed − contractual adjustments** (honestly DERIVED from real figures: the P1 contractual adjustments are insurer-agreed reductions that were never collectible; **write-offs are NOT subtracted** — uncollected-but-was-collectible, the standard "net" definition, stated). collections = `netCollectionsMinor` (P2 net allocations). Returns the real components (`collections_minor`, `charges_minor`, `contractual_adjustments_minor`, `collectible_minor`) + `rate` (fraction rounded to **4 decimals**; the page formats as %). **Zero-collectible period → `rate: null` ("—", NO divide-by-zero).** A rate > 1.0 (collecting prior-period balances) is reported honestly, never capped. **FENCE/HONESTY:** engine-computed from real figures (the page P6 displays, computes nothing); honest STATED definitions; collectible DERIVED (charges − contractual adjustments), NEVER fabricated; zero/edge cases → honest null ("—"), never a fabricated or divide-by-zero value; NO page-side arithmetic; NO visual this gate. **NOTHING weakened:** P1/P2/the ReconciliationEngine/the reconcile invariant intact; the methods READ real figures + write nothing. billing.view + tenant-scoped (fail-closed via BelongsToTenant). **NO existing behavior test modified.** Added `tests/Feature/Billing/DsoCollectionRateTest.php` (6 tests / 22 assertions, clock frozen so the period includes now): DSO = (AR/creditSales)×days from real figures (AR 30000 / sales 40000 × 30 days = **22.5**, exact components); a zero-sales period → **dso null** (components shown honestly, no divide-by-zero); net collection rate = collections/collectible (20000 / (30000−5000) = **0.8**); **collectible is DERIVED — a contractual adjustment reduces it by exactly its amount (30000→22000), a write-off does NOT (stays 22000)** — proving the honest definition + no fabrication; a zero-collectible period → **rate null**; billing.view-gated (reception refused on both) + tenant-scoped (a second tenant sees 0/null). Fourth fix part of the BILLING-AR chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/BILLING-AR-DIFF.md` §4 item #4 (now **RESOLVED** for the real dimension; the finer taxonomy stays a flagged gap). **Group by a REAL payer attribute; the groups TIE to the overall totals (δ=0, a real partition); engine-computed, page P6 displays, no page-side math. NO visual grid this gate.** **Opening:** top commit `a39d13b` (BILLAR.P3), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **THE PAYER DIMENSION (the key question — answered):** payer IS a real modeled attribute — `Invoice::payer_type` (string, REQUIRED, validated enum `assertPayerType`: `self_pay` | `private_insurance`) + nullable `payer_name` (insurer name); credit notes copy the source invoice's payer_type/payer_name. So the REAL dimension = `payer_type` with 2 categories. The wireframe's 4-way Swiss split (patient self-pay / supplementary insurance / accident SUVA-UVG / social municipal) is FINER than the model — NOT modeled — so I group by the real 2 + FLAG the finer taxonomy as a gap; I do NOT fabricate the 4 categories. **(1) The method** `MetricsService::arByPayer($actor,$from,$to): array` — groups AR + collections + charges by `payer_type` (billing.view, tenant-scoped, integer minor): the exhaustive partition key = the DISTINCT payer_types present on issued invoices (tenant-scoped); per group: AR = Σ `invoice_balances.open_balance_minor` over issued INV with that payer_type (the reconciled projection, consistent with `outstandingBalanceMinor`); collections = Σ net `PaymentAllocation`s in period whose invoice has that payer_type; charges = gross issued INV in period for that payer_type − credited (CN-cancelled) INV for that payer_type (the P2 net-of-credit-notes definition). Returns `groups[]` + `total_ar/collections/charges_minor` + `ties`. Scope-safe: subquery-filtered sums (Invoice/InvoiceBalance/PaymentAllocation global scopes), NO cross-tenant join. **(2) THE TIE (the crux):** the split is a REAL PARTITION — every issued invoice has exactly one payer_type, so Σ over groups of AR/collections/charges === the totals from the shared helpers (`outstandingBalanceMinor`/`netCollectionsMinor`/`chargesBilledMinor`), δ=0; `ties` confirms all three. The `billing.dunning` SETTINGS policy defines the level thresholds + fee_codes (the demo configures `fee_code => 'DUNNING-FEE'`). The current stage = `max(DunningEvent.level)` (the P7/audit source). So: events give level/date/status (real); the fee must be sourced from the real captured charges (NOT fabricated per event). **(1) The method** `MetricsService::accountDunning($actor, string $accountId, array $feeCodeByLevel, $asOf): array` (billing.view, tenant-scoped): reads the account's dunning_events (across the account's invoices) ordered by date/level → `events[]` `{invoice_id, invoice_number, level, triggered_on, status, fee_minor}`; `current_stage` = max(level); `reminder_count` = count; per-event `fee_minor` = the REAL captured fee Charge matched by (account, `$feeCodeByLevel[level]`, service_date == triggered_on) — 0 when the level has no configured fee; `fees_minor` = Σ those; `fees_tie` = `fees_minor` === Σ ALL the account's captured dunning-fee charges (the recorded fees — a non-tie would surface a fee charge with no matching event, i.e. drift). The `$feeCodeByLevel` map (level⇒fee_code) is derived by the CONTROLLER from the `billing.dunning` policy (`AccountDetailController::dunningFeeCodeByLevel` via SettingsService + `DunningService::SETTINGS_KEY`) — so MetricsService stays free of settings/policy coupling. **(2) The page** `Billing/AccountDetail.vue` adds a **dunning timeline** (a vertical timeline of the real events: level/date/status + the real fee) + a **stage pill** (the real max-level stage) + the account dunning figures (stage · reminders sent · Σ fees) — READ-ONLY, **NO dunning action button** (send-reminder + Betreibung escalation are deferred, carefully-gated). i18n additive `billing.accountDetail.dunning.*`. **FENCE:** the timeline + stage are the REAL persisted state machine (displayed, NOT page-computed — no client "if age>N"); read-only (append-only events, no mutation, no action route added this gate); the fee total ties to the real charges (δ=0). No fabricated dunning step/fee (the timeline surfaces only persisted events; the fee is the real captured Charge). **NOTHING weakened:** the DunningService state machine / P1 ledger / the ReconciliationEngine / the reconcile invariant intact; MetricsService gained a read-only method; the P1 AccountDetail render test still passes (the new `dunning` prop is additive). **NO existing behavior test modified.** Added `tests/Feature/Billing/AccountDunningTest.php` (5 tests / 41 assertions, run through the REAL `DunningService::evaluate` — a 61-day-overdue invoice + a policy with level-1 fee `DUN-FEE` 1500 + level-2 no-fee): the timeline reads the real events (levels [1,2], both on the run date, === the persisted rows) with per-event fee (level 1 = 1500, level 2 = 0); the stage === `max(DunningEvent.level)` (=2, the real machine); the fee total 1500 === Σ the real captured DUN-FEE charges (fees_tie true); the page renders the engine timeline (assertInertia — dunning.* === the service) AND **adds no mutation route** (asserted: every `billing/accounts` route is GET-only); an account with no dunning run shows an honest empty timeline (stage 0, fees 0, tie true); billing.view (reception 403) + tenant-scoped (cross-tenant 404). **BROWSER-VERIFIED** (Playwright, org_admin Andrea/2FA, a real dunning run): Roland Brunner's account shows the dunning timeline with the real events (level 1 + level 2) + the stage pill + fees, read-only (no action button). VERIFIED locally: `npm run build` green (`AccountDetail-*.js` 6.6→9.7 kB); `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK]`, **Pest 1144 passed**; `composer test:smoke` green. composer check + CI verified after push. Remaining AR-ACCOUNT-DETAIL parts (P3+): account-wide figures (last payment / total outstanding) + hero/status-pill/Swiss format · then record-payment (P4, PaymentService guard) / Betreibung escalation (operator-only + agent-excluded + audited) / payment plan.
